// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\components\reports\SF2ReportView.tsx

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
} from 'react-native';
import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext';
import { DepEdReportHeader, DepEdReportFooter } from './DepEdReportHeaderFooter';

export interface SchoolDay {
  dateNumber: number;
  dayOfWeek: 'M' | 'T' | 'W' | 'TH' | 'F';
  fullDate?: string;
}

export interface SF2Learner {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  name: string;
  sex: 'M' | 'F';
  attendance: { [dayNumber: number]: string | number };
  total_absent: number;
  total_tardy: number;
  remarks?: string;
}

export interface SF2ReportViewProps {
  data: any;
  learners?: any[];
}

export const SF2ReportView: React.FC<SF2ReportViewProps> = ({ data }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  if (!data) return null;

  // 1. Dynamic Faculty Signatory Resolution
  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const activeUserFullName =
    currentUser?.full_name ||
    `${currentUser?.first_name || ''} ${currentUser?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    currentUser?.faculty_name ||
    currentUser?.username ||
    '';

  const userRole = String(
    currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE'
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  const isTeachingRole = ['FACULTY', 'FACULTY', 'EMPLOYEE'].includes(userRole);

  const resolvedFacultyName =
    (isTeachingRole && activeUserFullName.length > 0
      ? activeUserFullName
      : null) ||
    data.adviser_name ||
    activeUserFullName ||
    'Class Adviser';

  const resolvedSchoolHead = String(data.school_head || 'School Head / Principal').trim();

  // 2. Safe Array Normalization
  const schoolDays: SchoolDay[] = Array.isArray(data.school_days) ? data.school_days : [];
  const males: SF2Learner[] = Array.isArray(data.males) ? data.males : [];
  const females: SF2Learner[] = Array.isArray(data.females) ? data.females : [];

  const standardSchoolDays = useMemo(() => {
    const list: SchoolDay[] = [];
    const maxDays = 25;
    const daysCycle: Array<'M' | 'T' | 'W' | 'TH' | 'F'> = ['M', 'T', 'W', 'TH', 'F'];

    for (let i = 0; i < maxDays; i++) {
      if (schoolDays[i]) {
        list.push(schoolDays[i]);
      } else {
        list.push({
          dateNumber: i + 1,
          dayOfWeek: daysCycle[i % 5],
        });
      }
    }
    return list;
  }, [schoolDays]);

  // 3. Totals Summary Cached with useMemo
  const totalsSummary = useMemo(() => {
    const maleAbsent = males.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_absent) || 0), 0);
    const maleTardy = males.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_tardy) || 0), 0);
    const femaleAbsent = females.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_absent) || 0), 0);
    const femaleTardy = females.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + (Number(cur.total_tardy) || 0), 0);

    return {
      maleAbsent,
      maleTardy,
      femaleAbsent,
      femaleTardy,
      combinedAbsent: maleAbsent + femaleAbsent,
      combinedTardy: maleTardy + femaleTardy,
    };
  }, [males, females]);

  // 4. Daily Column Tallies
  const dailyAttendanceTotals = useMemo(() => {
    const maxCols = 25;
    const malesCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);
    const femalesCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);
    const combinedCount: number[] = new Array(maxCols).fill(0);

    standardSchoolDays.forEach((day, idx) => {
      if (idx >= maxCols) return;
      let mPresent = 0;
      let fPresent = 0;

      males.forEach((m) => {
        const raw = String(m.attendance?.[day.dateNumber] ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (
          raw === 'present' ||
          raw === 'tardy' ||
          raw === 'late' ||
          raw === 'cutting' ||
          raw === '0' ||
          raw === ''
        ) {
          mPresent++;
        }
      });

      females.forEach((f) => {
        const raw = String(f.attendance?.[day.dateNumber] ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
        if (
          raw === 'present' ||
          raw === 'tardy' ||
          raw === 'late' ||
          raw === 'cutting' ||
          raw === '0' ||
          raw === ''
        ) {
          fPresent++;
        }
      });

      malesCount[idx] = mPresent;
      femalesCount[idx] = fPresent;
      combinedCount[idx] = mPresent + fPresent;
    });

    return { males: malesCount, females: femalesCount, combined: combinedCount };
  }, [standardSchoolDays, males, females]);

  // 5. Official DepEd Cell Notation Renderer
  const renderAttendanceCell = (status?: string | number | null) => {
    const s = String(status ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();

    if (s === 'absent' || s === 'x' || s === '1' || s === 'a') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <Text style={styles.textAbsent}>(x)</Text>
        </View>
      );
    }

    if (s === 'tardy' || s === 'late' || s === '2' || s === 't') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <View style={styles.triangleUpperShaded} />
        </View>
      );
    }

    if (s === 'cutting' || s === 'cc' || s === '3' || s === 'c') {
      return (
        <View style={styles.attCell}>
          <View style={styles.triangleLowerShaded} />
        </View>
      );
    }

    return <View style={styles.attCell} />;
  };

  const isListEmpty = males.length === 0 && females.length === 0;

  const metrics = data.metrics || {};
  const maleEnrolmentJune = metrics?.enrolment_june?.m ?? males.length;
  const femaleEnrolmentJune = metrics?.enrolment_june?.f ?? females.length;
  const totalEnrolmentJune = maleEnrolmentJune + femaleEnrolmentJune;

  const maleRegisteredEnd = males.length;
  const femaleRegisteredEnd = females.length;
  const totalRegisteredEnd = maleRegisteredEnd + femaleRegisteredEnd;

  const percentageEnrolment =
    totalEnrolmentJune > 0 ? ((totalRegisteredEnd / totalEnrolmentJune) * 100).toFixed(2) : '100.00';

  const adaM = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.m ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const adaF = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.f ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const adaTotal = Number(metrics?.average_daily_attendance?.total ?? (Number(adaM) + Number(adaF))).toFixed(1);

  const percAttM = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.m ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const percAttF = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.f ?? 0).toFixed(1);
  const percAttTotal = Number(metrics?.percentage_attendance?.total ?? 0).toFixed(1);

  const leftLogoUrl = data.left_logo || null;
  const rightLogoUrl = data.right_logo || null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.sheetOuterWrapper}>
      <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={true}>
        <View style={styles.officialDocumentCanvas}>
          <DepEdReportHeader
            leftLogoUrl={leftLogoUrl}
            rightLogoUrl={rightLogoUrl}
            region={data.region}
            division={data.division}
            formTitle="School Form 2 (SF2) Daily Attendance Report of Learners"
            formSubtitle="(This replaced Form 1, Form 2 & STS Form 4- Absenteeism and Dropout Profile)"
          />

          <View style={styles.metaTable}>
            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>School ID </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.school_id || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>School Year </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.academic_year || data.school_year || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.6 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Report for the Month of </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.month || '—'} {data.year || ''}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.metaRow, { borderTopWidth: 1, borderTopColor: '#000000' }]}>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.8 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Name of School </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.school_name || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Grade Level </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.grade_level || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.metaCol, { flex: 1.2 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Section </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.metaValue}>{data.section_name || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.matrixTable}>
            <View style={styles.tableRowHeader}>
              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>#</Text>
              </View>
              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 175, paddingHorizontal: 3 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>LEARNER'S NAME</Text>
                <Text style={styles.thSubText}>(Last Name, First Name, Middle Name)</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.daysContainer}>
                <View style={styles.daysTopBanner}>
                  <Text style={styles.daysBannerText}>
                    (1st row for date, 2nd row for Day: M,T,W,TH,F)
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.daysRowNumbers}>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`dnum-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayColCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.dayNumText}>{sd.dateNumber}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                </View>
                <View style={styles.daysRowLetters}>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`dday-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayColCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.dayLetterText}>{sd.dayOfWeek}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 70 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>Total for the{'\n'}Month</Text>
                <View style={styles.absTarSubRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.absTarCell, { borderRightWidth: 1, borderColor: '#000000' }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.thSmall}>ABSENT</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.absTarCell}>
                    <Text style={styles.thSmall}>TARDY</Text>
                  </View>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={[styles.thCell, { width: 155, paddingHorizontal: 4 }]}>
                <Text style={styles.thText}>
                  REMARK/S (If DROPPED OUT, state reason, please refer to legend number 2.{'\n'}
                  If TRANSFERRED IN/OUT, write the name of School.)
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            {isListEmpty ? (
              <View style={styles.emptyBox}>
                <Text style={styles.emptyText}>
                  {data.notice || `No student attendance records found for Section ${data.section_name}.`}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <>
                {males.map((m, rowIdx) => (
                  <View key={`male-row-${rowIdx}-${m.id || rowIdx}`} style={styles.tableRowData}>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdText}>{rowIdx + 1}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 175, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdLearnerName} numberOfLines={1}>{m.name}</Text>
                    </View>

                    {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => {
                      const status = m.attendance?.[sd.dateNumber];
                      return (
                        <View key={`m-${m.id || rowIdx}-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                          {renderAttendanceCell(status)}
                        </View>
                      );
                    })}

                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalAbsent}>{m.total_absent || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalTardy}>{m.total_tardy || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdRemarks} numberOfLines={1}>{m.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                ))}

                <View style={styles.tallyRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyLabel}>&larr; MALE | TOTAL Per Day &rarr;</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`m-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{dailyAttendanceTotals.males[colIdx] || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.maleAbsent || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.maleTardy || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>

                {females.map((f, rowIdx) => (
                  <View key={`female-row-${rowIdx}-${f.id || rowIdx}`} style={styles.tableRowData}>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 26 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdText}>{rowIdx + 1}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 175, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdLearnerName} numberOfLines={1}>{f.name}</Text>
                    </View>

                    {standardSchoolDays.map((sd, colIdx) => {
                      const status = f.attendance?.[sd.dateNumber];
                      return (
                        <View key={`f-${f.id || rowIdx}-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                          {renderAttendanceCell(status)}
                        </View>
                      );
                    })}

                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalAbsent}>{f.total_absent || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdTotalTardy}>{f.total_tardy || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                    <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 4 }]}>
                      <Text style={styles.tdRemarks} numberOfLines={1}>{f.remarks || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>
                ))}

                <View style={styles.tallyRow}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyLabel}>&larr; FEMALE | TOTAL Per Day &rarr;</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`f-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{dailyAttendanceTotals.females[colIdx] || ''}</Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.femaleAbsent || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.tallyCountText}>{totalsSummary.femaleTardy || ''}</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>

                <View style={[styles.tallyRow, { backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' }]}>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 201, alignItems: 'flex-start', paddingLeft: 6 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyLabel, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>Combined TOTAL PER DAY</Text>
                  </View>
                  {standardSchoolDays.map((_, colIdx) => (
                    <View key={`c-tot-col-${colIdx}`} style={styles.dayDataCell}>
                      <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                        {dailyAttendanceTotals.combined[colIdx] || ''}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  ))}
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                      {totalsSummary.combinedAbsent || ''}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 35 }]}>
                    <Text style={[styles.tallyCountText, { fontWeight: '900' }]}>
                      {totalsSummary.combinedTardy || ''}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                  <View style={[styles.tdCell, { width: 155 }]} />
                </View>
              </>
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.bottomBlockContainer}>
            <View style={styles.guidelinesCol}>
              <Text style={styles.blockHeading}>GUIDELINES:</Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                1. The attendance shall be accomplished daily. Refer to the codes for checking learners' attendance.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                2. Dates shall be written in the preceding columns beside Learner's Name.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>3. To compute the following:</Text>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>a. Percentage of Enrolment =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Registered Learner as of End of the Month</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Enrolment as of 1st Friday of June</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.multiplyText}>x 100</Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>b. Average Daily Attendance =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Total Daily Attendance</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Number of School Days in reporting month</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.formulaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.formulaLabel}>c. Percentage of Attendance for the month =</Text>
                <View style={styles.fractionWrap}>
                  <Text style={styles.fractionNumerator}>Average daily attendance</Text>
                  <View style={styles.fractionBar} />
                  <Text style={styles.fractionDenominator}>Registered Learner as of End of the month</Text>
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.multiplyText}>x 100</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                4. Every End of the month, the class adviser will submit this form to the office of the principal for recording of summary table into the School Form 4. Once signed by the principal, this form should be returned to the adviser.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                5. The adviser will extend necessary intervention including but not limited to home visitation to learner/s that committed 5 consecutive days of absences or those with potentials of dropping out.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineP}>
                6. Attendance performance of learner is expected to reflect in Form 137 and Form 138 every grading period.
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.guidelineFooter}>
                Beginning of School Year cut-off report is every 1st Friday of School Calendar Days
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.codesAndReasonsCol}>
              <Text style={styles.blockHeading}>1. CODES FOR CHECKING ATTENDANCE</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>blank </Text>- Present
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>(x) </Text>- Absent
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>
                <Text style={{ fontWeight: '800' }}>Tardy </Text>(half shaded Upper for Late Comer, Lower for Cutting Classes)
              </Text>

              <View style={styles.legendVisualRow}>
                <View style={styles.legendBox}>
                  <View style={styles.triangleUpperShadedLegend} />
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.legendLabel}>Late Comer</Text>

                <View style={[styles.legendBox, { marginLeft: 12 }]}>
                  <View style={styles.triangleLowerShadedLegend} />
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.legendLabel}>Cutting Classes</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={[styles.blockHeading, { marginTop: 6 }]}>
                2. REASONS/CAUSES OF DROP-OUTS
              </Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>a. Domestic-Related Factors</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>a.1. Had to take care of siblings</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>a.2. Early marriage/pregnancy</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>a.3. Parents' attitude toward schooling</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>a.4. Family problems</Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>b. Individual-Related Factors</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.1. Illness</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.2. Overage</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.3. Death</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.4. Drug Abuse</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.5. Poor academic performance</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.6. Lack of interest/Distractions</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>b.7. Hunger/Malnutrition</Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>c. School-Related Factors</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>c.1. Faculty Factor</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>c.2. Physical condition of classroom</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>c.3. Peer influence</Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>d. Geographic/Environmental</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>d.1. Distance between home and school</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>d.2. Armed conflict (incl. Tribal wars &amp; clanfeuds)</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>d.3. Calamities/Disasters</Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>e. Financial-Related</Text>
              <Text style={styles.reasonItem}>e.1. Child labor, work</Text>

              <Text style={styles.reasonSection}>f. Others</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.summaryTableCol}>
              <View style={styles.summaryMonthHeader}>
                <Text style={styles.summaryMonthTitle}>
                  Month: <Text style={styles.summaryMonthVal}>{data.month || '—'}</Text>
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.summaryDaysTitle}>
                  No. of Days of Classes: <Text style={styles.summaryMonthVal}>{standardSchoolDays.length}</Text>
                </Text>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.sumTableWrap}>
                <View style={styles.sumTableRowHeader}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableColTitle, { flex: 2.2 }]}>Summary for the Month</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableColTitle, { flex: 0.6 }]}>M</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableColTitle, { flex: 0.6 }]}>F</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableColTitle, { flex: 0.8 }]}>TOTAL</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Enrolment as of (1st Friday of June)
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{maleEnrolmentJune}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{femaleEnrolmentJune}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{totalEnrolmentJune}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Late Enrollment during the month{'\n'}(beyond cut-off)
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.late_enrolment?.m ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.late_enrolment?.f ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
                    {metrics?.late_enrolment?.total ?? 0}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Registered Learner as of end of the month
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{maleRegisteredEnd}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{femaleRegisteredEnd}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{totalRegisteredEnd}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Percentage of Enrolment as of end of the month
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>—</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>—</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
                    {percentageEnrolment}%
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Average Daily Attendance
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{adaM}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{adaF}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{adaTotal}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Percentage of Attendance for the month
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{percAttM}%</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{percAttF}%</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{percAttTotal}%</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>
                    Number of students with 5 consecutive days of absences:
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.consecutive_5?.m ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.consecutive_5?.f ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>
                    {metrics?.consecutive_5?.total ?? 0}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>Drop out</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.drop_out?.m ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.drop_out?.f ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{metrics?.drop_out?.total ?? 0}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>Transferred out</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.transferred_out?.m ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.transferred_out?.f ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{metrics?.transferred_out?.total ?? 0}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sumTableRow}>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellLabel, { flex: 2.2 }]}>Transferred in</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.transferred_in?.m ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.6 }]}>{metrics?.transferred_in?.f ?? 0}</Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.sumTableCellVal, { flex: 0.8, fontWeight: '700' }]}>{metrics?.transferred_in?.total ?? 0}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.signSection}>
                <Text style={styles.certifyDeclaration}>
                  I certify that this is a true and correct report.
                </Text>

                <View style={styles.signFieldBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.signPersonName}>{resolvedFacultyName.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                  <View style={styles.signUnderline} />
                  <Text style={styles.signPositionLabel}>
                    (Signature of Faculty over Printed Name)
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={[styles.signFieldBox, { marginTop: 14 }]}>
                  <Text style={styles.attestLabel}>Attested by:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.signPersonName}>{resolvedSchoolHead.toUpperCase()}</Text>
                  <View style={styles.signUnderline} />
                  <Text style={styles.signPositionLabel}>
                    (Signature of School Head over Printed Name)
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>

          <DepEdReportFooter
            schoolLogoUrl={data.school_logo || data.right_logo || null}
            address={data.school_address || data.address}
            contactNumber={data.school_contact || data.contact_number}
            email={data.school_email || data.email}
          />
          <View style={styles.pageMetaFooter}>
            <Text style={styles.pageMetaText}>School Form 2: Page 2 of 2</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Modal, StyleSheet, Text, View, TextInput, Alert } from 'react-native';
import { getServerBaseUrl, setServerBaseUrl, resetServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';
import { Button } from './common/Button';
import { theme } from '../constants/theme';

interface ServerConfigModalProps {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ServerConfigModal({ visible, onClose }: ServerConfigModalProps) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      getServerBaseUrl().then(setUrl);
    }
  }, [visible]);

  const handleSave = async () => {
    if (!url.startsWith('http://') && !url.startsWith('https://')) {
      Alert.alert('Invalid URL', 'Address must begin with http:// or https://');
      return;
    }
    await setServerBaseUrl(url);
    Alert.alert('Saved', 'Server address updated successfully.');
    onClose();
  };

  const handleReset = async () => {
    await resetServerBaseUrl();
    const defaultUrl = await getServerBaseUrl();
    setUrl(defaultUrl);
    Alert.alert('Reset', 'Restored default server address.');
  };

  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade">
      <View style={styles.overlay}>
        <View style={styles.modalBox}>
          <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Server Connection Settings</Text>
          <Text style={styles.modalDesc}>
            Configure the AttendSure backend endpoint dynamically for local development or remote campus deployment.
          </Text>

          <Text style={styles.label}>Host Base URL</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={url}
            onChangeText={setUrl}
            placeholder="http://10.16.115.49:8000"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            autoCorrect={false}
          />

          <View style={styles.buttonRow}>
            <Button title="Reset Default" variant="outline" onPress={handleReset} style={styles.btnFlex} />
            <Button title="Save & Apply" variant="primary" onPress={handleSave} style={styles.btnFlex} />
          </View>
          <Button title="Cancel" variant="secondary" onPress={onClose} style={styles.cancelBtn} />
        </View>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  overlay: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.6)',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: theme.spacing.lg,
  },
  modalBox: {
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.lg,
    padding: theme.spacing.lg,
  },
  modalTitle: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: '700',
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
  },
  modalDesc: {
    fontSize: 12,
    color: theme.colors.textSecondary,
    marginVertical: theme.spacing.xs,
    lineHeight: 18,
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 12,
    fontWeight: '600',
    color: theme.colors.textPrimary,
    marginTop: theme.spacing.sm,
    marginBottom: 4,
  },
  input: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: theme.colors.borderStrong,
    borderRadius: theme.borderRadius.md,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    fontSize: 13,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceSubtle,
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  buttonRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 8,
  },
  btnFlex: {
    flex: 1,
    height: 40,
  },
  cancelBtn: {
    marginTop: 8,

# FILE: mobile\src\context\AuthContext.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Authentication Context & Session Manager
 * File: mobile/src/context/AuthContext.tsx
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { apiClient, AUTH_TOKEN_KEY } from '../api/client';

const USER_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_user_profile';

export interface AuthenticatedUser {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name: string;
  faculty_name: string;
  faculty_id: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
  system_role: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string | null;
  division?: string | null;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  photo?: string | null;
  avatar?: string | null;
  school_name?: string;
  qr_token?: string | null;
  bound_device_id?: string | null;
  is_superuser: boolean;
  is_staff: boolean;
}

interface AuthContextType {
  user: AuthenticatedUser | null;
  token: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  login: (username: string, password: string) => Promise<void>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshUser: () => Promise<void>;
  updateUser: (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>) => Promise<void>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const saveTokenSafely = async (token: string): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.setItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    } catch {}
  }
};

const clearTokenSafely = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await AsyncStorage.multiRemove([AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, USER_CACHE_KEY]);
  if (Platform.OS !== 'web') {
    try {
      await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
    } catch {}
  }
};

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<AuthenticatedUser | null>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const isRefreshingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);

  const refreshUser = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isRefreshingRef.current) return;
    isRefreshingRef.current = true;

    try {
      const storedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
      if (!storedToken) {
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
        return;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.get('/api/me/');
      if (res.data && res.data.id) {
        setUser(res.data);
        setToken(storedToken);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      const status = err?.response?.status;
      if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
      }
    } finally {
      isRefreshingRef.current = false;
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const restoreSession = async () => {
      try {
        const storedToken = await AsyncStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
        const cachedUserStr = await AsyncStorage.getItem(USER_CACHE_KEY);

        if (storedToken && cachedUserStr) {
          setToken(storedToken);
          setUser(JSON.parse(cachedUserStr));
        }

        if (storedToken) {
          await refreshUser();
        }
      } catch {
        await clearTokenSafely();
        setUser(null);
        setToken(null);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    restoreSession();
  }, [refreshUser]);

  const login = async (username: string, password: string): Promise<void> => {
    const cleanUsername = username.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();
    const cleanPassword = password.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();

    if (!cleanUsername || !cleanPassword) {
      throw new Error('Please enter both username and password.');
    }

    const res = await apiClient.post('/api/auth/login/', {
      username: cleanUsername,
      password: cleanPassword,
    });

    const d = res.data || {};
    const receivedToken: string | undefined = d.token || d.access || d.key;
    const receivedUser: AuthenticatedUser | undefined = d.user || (d.id ? d : undefined);

    if (!receivedToken || !receivedUser) {
      throw new Error('Invalid response received from authentication server.');
    }

    await saveTokenSafely(receivedToken);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(receivedUser));

    setToken(receivedToken);
    setUser(receivedUser);

    refreshUser().catch(() => {});
  };

  const logout = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await clearTokenSafely();
    setToken(null);
    setUser(null);
  };

  const updateUser = async (updatedFields: Partial<AuthenticatedUser>): Promise<void> => {
    if (!user) return;
    const nextUser = { ...user, ...updatedFields };
    setUser(nextUser);
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(USER_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextUser));
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        token,
        loading,
        isLoading: loading,
        login,
        logout,
        refreshUser,
        updateUser,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = (): AuthContextType => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

export default AuthContext;

# FILE: mobile\src\screens\CalendarScreen.tsx
================================================================================
// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\CalendarScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Switch,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  RefreshControl,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

const CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_calendar_data';

interface CalendarException {
  id: number;
  type: 'SUSPENSION' | 'HOLIDAY' | 'EVENT';
  date: string;
  reason: string;
  affects_all_grades?: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
}

const getLocalTodayDateString = (): string => {
  const now = new Date();
  const year = now.getFullYear();
  const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

export const CalendarScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const rawRole = String(currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const isAdmin =
    rawRole === 'ADMIN' ||
    rawRole === 'PRINCIPAL' ||
    (Boolean(currentUser?.is_superuser) && rawRole !== 'FACULTY');

  const [weekendRestEnabled, setWeekendRestEnabled] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<'SUSPENSION' | 'HOLIDAY' | 'EVENT'>('SUSPENSION');
  const [exceptionDate, setExceptionDate] = useState<string>(getLocalTodayDateString);
  const [exceptionReason, setExceptionReason] = useState<string>('');
  const [exceptions, setExceptions] = useState<CalendarException[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // 1. Cache-First Initialization
  const loadCachedCalendar = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY);
      if (cached && isMounted.current) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(cached);
        if (typeof parsed.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
          setWeekendRestEnabled(parsed.weekend_rest_enabled);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(parsed.exceptions)) {
          setExceptions(parsed.exceptions);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Live network fetch fallback
    }
  }, []);

  // 2. Stable Data Fetching Callback
  const fetchCalendarData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('/calendar/exceptions/', {
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      const payload = res.data;

      if (!isMounted.current) return;

      let nextWeekendRest = true;
      let nextExceptions: CalendarException[] = [];

      if (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) {
        if (typeof payload.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
          nextWeekendRest = payload.weekend_rest_enabled;
          setWeekendRestEnabled(payload.weekend_rest_enabled);
        }
        nextExceptions = Array.isArray(payload.results) ? payload.results : [];
      } else if (Array.isArray(payload)) {
        nextExceptions = payload;
      }

      setExceptions(nextExceptions);

      await AsyncStorage.setItem(
        CACHED_CALENDAR_KEY,
        JSON.stringify({
          weekend_rest_enabled: nextWeekendRest,
          exceptions: nextExceptions,
        })
      );
    } catch {
      // Ignore cancellations
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    loadCachedCalendar().then(() => {
      fetchCalendarData();
    });

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) {
        abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      }
    };
  }, [loadCachedCalendar, fetchCalendarData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchCalendarData();
  }, [fetchCalendarData]);

  // 3. Weekend Rest Toggle
  const handleToggleWeekendRest = async (value: boolean) => {
    if (!isAdmin) return;
    const previousValue = weekendRestEnabled;
    setWeekendRestEnabled(value);

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.patch('/calendar/exceptions/', {
        weekend_rest_enabled: value,
      });
      if (typeof res.data?.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
        setWeekendRestEnabled(res.data.weekend_rest_enabled);
      }
    } catch {
      setWeekendRestEnabled(previousValue);
      showAlert('Update Failed', 'Could not save weekend rest setting.');
    }
  };

  // 4. Input Sanitization & Override Submission
  const handleAddException = async () => {
    if (!isAdmin) {
      showAlert('Denied', 'Only school administrators can set calendar overrides.');
      return;
    }

    const cleanReason = exceptionReason.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim();
    const cleanDate = exceptionDate.trim();

    if (!cleanReason) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please enter a description or reason.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanReason.length > 255) {
      showAlert('Invalid Input', 'Reason cannot exceed 255 characters.');
      return;
    }

    // Strict Date Validation (YYYY-MM-DD)
    const dateRegex = /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])$/;
    if (!dateRegex.test(cleanDate)) {
      showAlert('Invalid Date', 'Date format must be YYYY-MM-DD.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        type: selectedType,
        date: cleanDate,
        reason: cleanReason,
        affects_all_grades: true,
      };

      const res = await apiClient.post('/calendar/exceptions/', payload);
      if (res.data && res.data.id) {
        setExceptions((prev) => [res.data, ...prev.filter((i) => i.id !== res.data.id)]);
      } else {
        await fetchCalendarData();
      }

      setExceptionReason('');
      showAlert('Saved', 'Calendar override saved.');
    } catch (err: any) {
      const rawError = String(err?.response?.data?.error || err?.response?.data?.detail || '');
      const safeError = rawError.includes('Traceback')
        ? 'Could not save override. Please check server.'
        : rawError || 'Failed to save calendar override.';
      showAlert('Save Failed', safeError);
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setSubmitting(false);
      }
    }
  };

  // 5. Deletion Handler with Numeric ID Validation
  const executeDeleteException = async (id: number) => {
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) return;

    try {
      await apiClient.delete(`/calendar/exceptions/${id}/`);
      setExceptions((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
    } catch {
      showAlert('Error', 'Could not remove override.');
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteException = (id: number) => {
    if (!isAdmin) {
      showAlert('Denied', 'Only school administrators can remove overrides.');
      return;
    }

    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      if (window.confirm('Remove this calendar override?')) {
        executeDeleteException(id);
      }
      return;
    }

    Alert.alert('Confirm Delete', 'Remove this calendar override?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Delete',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: () => executeDeleteException(id),
      },
    ]);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <View>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>Calendar</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>
            {isAdmin ? 'Date Overrides & Schedules' : 'Schedules & Memorandums'}
          </Text>
        </View>
        <View style={[styles.headerBadge, !isAdmin && styles.headerBadgeFaculty]}>
          <View style={[styles.activeDot, !isAdmin && { backgroundColor: '#16a34a' }]} />
          <Text style={[styles.headerBadgeText, !isAdmin && { color: '#16a34a' }]}>
            {isAdmin ? 'ADMIN' : 'DEPED'}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Weekend Rest Card */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.weekendCardRow}>
            <View style={styles.cardIconBox}>
              <Ionicons name="calendar" size={20} color={COLORS.primary} />
            </View>
            <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 10 }}>
              <Text style={styles.cardHeading}>Weekend Rest</Text>
              <Text style={styles.cardDetailText}>
                Saturdays and Sundays are excluded from attendance logging
              </Text>
            </View>
            {isAdmin ? (
              <Switch
                value={weekendRestEnabled}
                onValueChange={handleToggleWeekendRest}
                trackColor={{ false: '#cbd5e1', true: '#b8d6f0' }}
                thumbColor={weekendRestEnabled ? COLORS.primary : '#f8fafc'}
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.readOnlyPill}>
                <Text style={styles.readOnlyPillText}>
                  {weekendRestEnabled ? 'ACTIVE' : 'OFF'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Admin Form: Add Override */}
        {isAdmin ? (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>ADD OVERRIDE</Text>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Effective Date (YYYY-MM-DD)</Text>
              <View style={styles.inputBox}>
                <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.inputText}
                  value={exceptionDate}
                  onChangeText={setExceptionDate}
                  placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD"
                  placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  maxLength={10}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Category</Text>
              <View style={styles.typeSelectorRow}>
                {(['SUSPENSION', 'HOLIDAY', 'EVENT'] as const).map((type) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={type}
                    style={[styles.typePill, selectedType === type && styles.typePillActive]}
                    onPress={() => setSelectedType(type)}
                    activeOpacity={0.8}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.typePillText, selectedType === type && styles.typePillTextActive]}>
                      {type}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.fieldRow}>
              <Text style={styles.fieldLabel}>Reason / Memorandum</Text>
              <View style={[styles.inputBox, { height: 44 }]}>
                <Ionicons name="document-text-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <TextInput
                  style={styles.inputText}
                  placeholder="Official memorandum or reason"
                  placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
                  value={exceptionReason}
                  onChangeText={setExceptionReason}
                  maxLength={255}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.addOverrideBtn, submitting && styles.btnDisabled]}
              onPress={handleAddException}
              disabled={submitting}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Ionicons name="add-circle-outline" size={16} color="#ffffff" style={{ marginRight: 6 }} />
                  <Text style={styles.addOverrideBtnText}>Save</Text>
                </>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.infoBanner}>
            <Ionicons name="information-circle-outline" size={18} color="#004b87" />
            <Text style={styles.infoBannerText}>
              Official class suspensions and calendar overrides are managed by administrators.
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Active Overrides List */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.listHeaderRow}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>
              ACTIVE OVERRIDES ({exceptions.length})
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.listSubText}>Overrides regular schedule</Text>
          </View>

          {loading ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} style={{ marginVertical: 14 }} />
          ) : exceptions.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.noExceptionsText}>No active date overrides set.</Text>
          ) : (
            exceptions.map((item) => (
              <View key={String(item.id)} style={styles.exceptionItemRow}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.typeBadge,
                    item.type === 'SUSPENSION'
                      ? styles.badgeSuspension
                      : item.type === 'HOLIDAY'
                      ? styles.badgeHoliday
                      : styles.badgeEvent,
                  ]}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.typeBadgeText,
                      item.type === 'SUSPENSION'
                        ? styles.textSuspension
                        : item.type === 'HOLIDAY'
                        ? styles.textHoliday
                        : styles.textEvent,
                    ]}
                  >
                    {item.type}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={{ flex: 1, marginHorizontal: 12 }}>
                  <Text style={styles.exceptionDateText}>{item.date}</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.exceptionReasonText}>{item.reason}</Text>
                </View>

                {isAdmin && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => handleDeleteException(item.id)}
                    style={styles.deleteBtn}
                    activeOpacity={0.7}
                  >
                    <Ionicons name="trash-outline" size={16} color="#ef4444" />
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            ))
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  topHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',

  },

================================================================================
# FILE: mobile\src\screens\ChatScreen.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  FlatList,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';

interface ThreadItem {
  thread_key: string;
  student_id: number;
  student_name: string;
  section: string;
  faculty_id: number;
  faculty_name: string;
  parent_name: string;
  last_message: string;
  last_time: string;
  unread_count: number;
}

interface MessageItem {
  id: number;
  sender_name: string;
  is_mine: boolean;
  body: string;
  time_only: string;
}

const getInitials = (name: string): string => {
  const parts = String(name || '').replace(/,/g, '').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length >= 2) return `${parts[0][0]}${parts[1][0]}`.toUpperCase();
  return String(name || 'FA').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
};

export const ChatScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();

  const [threads, setThreads] = useState<ThreadItem[]>([]);
  const [activeThread, setActiveThread] = useState<ThreadItem | null>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<MessageItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState<string>('');
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<'ALL' | 'UNREAD'>('ALL');
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<string>('');
  const [sending, setSending] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList<MessageItem> | null>(null);

  const loadThreads = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<any>('chat/threads/');
      setThreads(Array.isArray(res.data?.threads) ? res.data.threads : []);
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadMessages = useCallback(async (thread: ThreadItem) => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<any>('chat/messages/', {
        params: { student_id: thread.student_id, faculty_id: thread.faculty_id },
      });
      setMessages(Array.isArray(res.data?.messages) ? res.data.messages : []);
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadThreads();
  }, [loadThreads]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeThread) return;
    loadMessages(activeThread);
    const timer = setInterval(() => loadMessages(activeThread), 8000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [activeThread, loadMessages]);

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!activeThread || !draft.trim() || sending) return;
    const text = draft.trim();
    setDraft('');
    setSending(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post<MessageItem>('chat/messages/', {
        student_id: activeThread.student_id,
        faculty_id: activeThread.faculty_id,
        body: text,
      });
      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, res.data]);
      setTimeout(() => flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true }), 100);
    } catch {
      setDraft(text);
    } finally {
      setSending(false);
    }
  };

  const totalUnread = threads.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.unread_count || 0), 0);

  const filteredThreads = threads.filter((t) => {
    if (filterMode === 'UNREAD' && t.unread_count <= 0) return false;
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return true;
    return (
      t.student_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.faculty_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.parent_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      t.section.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  });

  if (!activeThread) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
        {/* Royal Blue Curved Header */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.royalHeader,
            { backgroundColor: palette.headerBg, paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Messages</Text>
              <Text style={styles.headerSub}>
                {totalUnread === 0 ? 'You are all caught up' : `${totalUnread} unread message(s)`}
              </Text>
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.composeSquare} onPress={loadThreads}>
              <Ionicons name="create-outline" size={20} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Search Input & Filter Row */}
        <View style={styles.controlsWrap}>
          <View style={styles.searchRow}>
            <View style={[styles.searchBox, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border }]}>
              <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} />
              <TextInput
                style={[styles.searchInput, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}
                value={search}
                onChangeText={setSearch}
                placeholder="Search faculty or parents"
                placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
              />
            </View>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.filterIconBtn, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border }]}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode(filterMode === 'ALL' ? 'UNREAD' : 'ALL')}
            >
              <Ionicons name="options-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.pillsRow}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.filterPill,
                filterMode === 'ALL'
                  ? { backgroundColor: palette.primary }
                  : { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border, borderWidth: 1 },
              ]}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode('ALL')}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.filterPillText, { color: filterMode === 'ALL' ? '#FFFFFF' : palette.textSecondary }]}>
                All
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[
                styles.filterPill,
                filterMode === 'UNREAD'
                  ? { backgroundColor: palette.primary }
                  : { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border, borderWidth: 1 },
              ]}
              onPress={() => setFilterMode('UNREAD')}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.filterPillText, { color: filterMode === 'UNREAD' ? '#FFFFFF' : palette.textSecondary }]}>
                Unread
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>

        {loading ? (
          <View style={styles.centerBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={palette.primary} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredThreads}
            keyExtractor={(item) => item.thread_key}
            contentContainerStyle={{ paddingHorizontal: 16, paddingBottom: 120 }}
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <View style={styles.centerBox}>
                <Ionicons name="chatbubbles-outline" size={44} color={palette.textMuted} />
                <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>No conversations found</Text>
                <Text style={[styles.mutedText, { color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
                  Linked parent and faculty chat threads appear here automatically.
                </Text>
              </View>
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.threadRow}
                onPress={() => setActiveThread(item)}
                activeOpacity={0.75}
              >
                <View style={[styles.initialsCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft }]}>
                  <Text style={[styles.initialsText, { color: palette.primary }]}>
                    {getInitials(item.faculty_name || item.student_name)}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text style={[styles.threadTitle, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                    {item.faculty_name || item.parent_name}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={[styles.threadSub, { color: palette.textSecondary }]} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {item.student_name} • {item.section}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {item.unread_count > 0 && (
                  <View style={[styles.unreadBadge, { backgroundColor: palette.primary }]}>
                    <Text style={styles.unreadText}>{item.unread_count}</Text>
                  </View>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          />
        )}
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={80}
    >
      <View
        style={[
          styles.royalHeader,
          { backgroundColor: palette.headerBg, paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14) },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.headerTopRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              setActiveThread(null);
              loadThreads();
            }}
            style={styles.composeSquare}
          >
            <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color="#FFFFFF" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: 12 }}>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              {activeThread.student_name}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSub} numberOfLines={1}>
              {activeThread.section} • {activeThread.faculty_name}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      <FlatList
        ref={flatListRef}
        data={messages}
        keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
        contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, paddingBottom: 110, gap: 8 }}
        onContentSizeChange={() => flatListRef.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: false })}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.msgBubble,
              item.is_mine
                ? { alignSelf: 'flex-end', backgroundColor: palette.primary }
                : { alignSelf: 'flex-start', backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border, borderWidth: 1 },
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.senderMeta, { color: item.is_mine ? '#DBE4FF' : palette.textMuted }]}>
              {item.sender_name} • {item.time_only}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.msgText, { color: item.is_mine ? '#FFFFFF' : palette.textPrimary }]}>
              {item.body}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
      />

      <View style={[styles.inputRow, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.border, marginBottom: 78 }]}>
        <TextInput
          style={[styles.inputBox, { backgroundColor: palette.background, color: palette.textPrimary }]}
          value={draft}
          onChangeText={setDraft}
          placeholder="Type a message..."
          placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
        />
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.sendBtn, { backgroundColor: palette.primary }, (!draft.trim() || sending) && { opacity: 0.5 }]}
          onPress={handleSend}
          disabled={!draft.trim() || sending}
        >
          <Ionicons name="send" size={18} color="#FFFFFF" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1 },
  royalHeader: {
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    paddingBottom: 22,
    borderBottomLeftRadius: 28,
    borderBottomRightRadius: 28,

    flexDirection: 'row',

    gap: 8,

  filterPill: {

    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderRadius: 20,
  },
  filterPillText: {

    fontWeight: '700',
  },
  threadRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingVertical: 12,
    gap: 14,
  },
  initialsCircle: {
    width: 50,
    height: 50,
    borderRadius: 25,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  initialsText: {
    fontSize: 15,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  threadTitle: {

  },

  },

  },

    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 8,
    padding: 10,
    borderTopWidth: 1,
  },
  inputBox: {
    flex: 1,
    borderRadius: 20,
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    fontSize: 14,
  },
  sendBtn: {
    width: 42,
    height: 42,
    borderRadius: 21,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
});

export default ChatScreen;

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';

  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,

  Vibration,
  Modal,
} from 'react-native';

const fallbackLogo = require('../../assets/AttendSureLogo.svg');

function calculateDistanceMeters(
  lat1: number,
  lon1: number,
  lat2: number,
  lon2: number
): number {
  const R = 6371000.0;
  const dLat = ((lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const dLon = ((lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const radLat1 = (lat1 * Math.PI) / 180.0;
  const radLat2 = (lat2 * Math.PI) / 180.0;

  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.cos(radLat1) * Math.cos(radLat2);
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
  return Math.round(R * c);
}

function formatDistance(meters: number): string {
  if (meters >= 1000) {
    return `${(meters / 1000).toFixed(2)} km`;
  }
  return `${Math.round(meters)} m`;
}

interface StudentItem {
  id: number;
  lrn: string;
  qr_token?: string;
  full_name: string;
  gender?: string;
  attendance_status: string;
}

interface SectionItem {
  schedule_id: number;
  section_id: number;
  section_name: string;
  subject_name: string;
  subject_code: string;
  room: string;
  start_time: string;
  end_time: string;
  total_students: number;
  students: StudentItem[];
}

interface SchoolProfileData {
  school_id?: string;
  school_name?: string;
  division?: string;
  region?: string;
  school_logo?: string | null;
  left_logo?: string | null;
  latitude?: number | null;
  longitude?: number | null;
  geofence_radius_meters?: number | null;
}

interface DtrDayRow {
  day: number;
  date_str: string;
  day_of_week: string;
  is_weekend: boolean;
  am_arrival: string;
  am_departure: string;
  pm_arrival: string;
  pm_departure: string;
  undertime_hours: string;
  undertime_minutes: string;
}

interface DtrSummaryData {
  faculty_id: number;
  faculty_name: string;
  employee_id: string;
  department: string;
  month: string;
  year: number;
  days_present: number;
  total_undertime_hours: number;
  total_undertime_minutes: number;
  dtr_submitted: boolean;
  dept_head_approved: boolean;
  rows: DtrDayRow[];
}

interface GeofenceConfigData {

  latitude: number | null;
  longitude: number | null;
  radius_meters: number | null;
  is_configured: boolean;
}

interface CalendarOverrideItem {
  id: number;
  date: string;
  type: 'SUSPENSION' | 'HOLIDAY' | 'EVENT';
  reason: string;
}

  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
