  mocked: boolean;
  timestamp: string;
}

interface DashboardProps {
  onNavigate?: (tab: TabType) => void;
}

const getLocalTodayStr = (): string => {
  const now = new Date();
  const y = now.getFullYear();
  const m = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
};

const getMindfulGreeting = (): { greeting: string; thought: string } => {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  if (hour < 12) {
    return {
      greeting: 'Good morning',
      thought: 'Presence is the highest form of dedication to your learners.',
    };
  }
  if (hour < 17) {
    return {
      greeting: 'Good afternoon',
      thought: 'Excellence in service through daily consistency and care.',
    };
  }
  return {
    greeting: 'Good evening',
    thought: 'Reflecting on a day of purpose, integrity, and mentorship.',
  };
};

export const DashboardScreen: React.FC<DashboardProps> = ({ onNavigate }) => {
  const { user, refreshUser } = useAuth();

  const [sections, setSections] = useState<SectionItem[]>([]);
  const [schoolProfile, setSchoolProfile] = useState<SchoolProfileData | null>(null);
  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<DtrSummaryData | null>(null);
  const [geofenceConfig, setGeofenceConfig] = useState<GeofenceConfigData | null>(null);
  const [todayOverride, setTodayOverride] = useState<CalendarOverrideItem | null>(null);
  const [weekendRestEnabled, setWeekendRestEnabled] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [isSyncingQueue, setIsSyncingQueue] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [offlineCount, setOfflineCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [lastSyncTime, setLastSyncTime] = useState<string>('');
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState<string>('');
  const [logoFailed, setLogoFailed] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [avatarFailed, setAvatarFailed] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [liveCoords, setLiveCoords] = useState<LiveGpsCoords | null>(null);
  const [gpsError, setGpsError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [showAdvisoryModal, setShowAdvisoryModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [advisoryAcknowledged, setAdvisoryAcknowledged] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const lastAdvisoryTimestamp = useRef<number>(0);

  const isMounted = useRef(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const anchorCoordsRef = useRef<LiveGpsCoords | null>(null);
  const lastHeartbeatSent = useRef<number>(0);

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const displayName =
    currentUser?.full_name ||
    `${currentUser?.first_name || ''} ${currentUser?.last_name || ''}`.trim() ||
    currentUser?.faculty_name ||
    currentUser?.username ||
    '';

  const employeeId =
    currentUser?.employee_id ||
    currentUser?.faculty_id ||
    dtrData?.employee_id ||
    currentUser?.username ||
    '';

  const schoolName =
    schoolProfile?.school_name ||
    currentUser?.school_name ||
    geofenceConfig?.name ||
    '';

  const schoolSubHeader = [
    schoolProfile?.school_id ? `School ID: ${schoolProfile.school_id}` : '',
    schoolProfile?.division || '',
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' • ');

  const mindfulQuote = useMemo(() => getMindfulGreeting(), []);

  const resolvePhotoUrl = useCallback((path: string | null | undefined, base: string) => {
    if (!path) return null;
    if (path.startsWith('http://') || path.startsWith('https://') || path.startsWith('data:')) {
      return path;
    }
    const cleanBase = base.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    const cleanPath = path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`;
    return `${cleanBase}${cleanPath}`;
  }, []);

  const rawUserPhoto =
    currentUser?.photo_url ||
    currentUser?.photo ||
    currentUser?.avatar_url ||
    currentUser?.avatar ||
    null;
  const resolvedUserPhoto = resolvePhotoUrl(rawUserPhoto, serverBase);

  useEffect(() => {
    startPassivePresenceTracking().catch((err) =>
      console.log('[BackgroundGPS] Startup notice:', err?.message || err)
    );
  }, [currentUser?.username, currentUser?.id]);

  const applyStationaryFilter = useCallback((raw: LiveGpsCoords): LiveGpsCoords => {
    const anchor = anchorCoordsRef.current;
    if (!anchor) {
      anchorCoordsRef.current = raw;
      return raw;
    }

    const displacement = calculateDistanceMeters(
      anchor.latitude,
      anchor.longitude,
      raw.latitude,
      raw.longitude
    );

    if (displacement < 6) {
      return {
        latitude: anchor.latitude,
        longitude: anchor.longitude,
        accuracy: raw.accuracy ?? anchor.accuracy,
        mocked: raw.mocked,
        timestamp: raw.timestamp,
      };
    }

    anchorCoordsRef.current = raw;
    return raw;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let watcherSubscription: Location.LocationSubscription | null = null;
    let heartbeatIntervalTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
    let isSubscribed = true;

    const transmitHeartbeat = async (coords: LiveGpsCoords) => {
      try {
        await sendLiveHeartbeat(coords);
        if (isSubscribed) {
          setIsOnline(true);
          setGpsError(null);
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        if (isSubscribed) {
          const msg = err?.message || '';
          if (msg.includes('locked') || msg.includes('mismatch') || msg.includes('403')) {
            setGpsError(msg);
            setIsOnline(false);
          }
        }
      }
    };

    const startLiveGpsTracking = async () => {
      try {
        const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
        if (status !== 'granted') {
          if (isSubscribed) setGpsError('Location permission needed for attendance.');
          return;
        }

        const isGpsActive = await Location.hasServicesEnabledAsync();
        if (!isGpsActive) {
          if (isSubscribed) setGpsError('Phone GPS is turned off.');
          return;
        }

        if (isSubscribed) setGpsError(null);

        const initial = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({
          accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High,
        });

        if (isSubscribed && initial) {
          const rawCoords: LiveGpsCoords = {
            latitude: initial.coords.latitude,
            longitude: initial.coords.longitude,
            accuracy: initial.coords.accuracy,
            mocked: Boolean(initial.mocked),
            timestamp: format12HourTime(new Date()),
          };

          const stabilized = applyStationaryFilter(rawCoords);
          setLiveCoords(stabilized);

          lastHeartbeatSent.current = Date.now();
          transmitHeartbeat(stabilized);
        }

        watcherSubscription = await Location.watchPositionAsync(
          {
            accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High,
            timeInterval: 5000,
            distanceInterval: 3,
          },
          (position) => {
            if (!isSubscribed) return;

            const rawCoords: LiveGpsCoords = {
              latitude: position.coords.latitude,
              longitude: position.coords.longitude,
              accuracy: position.coords.accuracy,
              mocked: Boolean(position.mocked),
              timestamp: format12HourTime(new Date()),
            };

            const stabilized = applyStationaryFilter(rawCoords);
            setLiveCoords(stabilized);
            setGpsError(null);

            const now = Date.now();
            if (now - lastHeartbeatSent.current >= 60000) {
              lastHeartbeatSent.current = now;
              transmitHeartbeat(stabilized);
            }
          }
        );

        heartbeatIntervalTimer = setInterval(async () => {
          if (!isSubscribed) return;
          const now = Date.now();
          if (now - lastHeartbeatSent.current >= 60000) {
            try {
              const freshLoc = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High });
              const rawCoords: LiveGpsCoords = {
                latitude: freshLoc.coords.latitude,
                longitude: freshLoc.coords.longitude,
                accuracy: freshLoc.coords.accuracy,
                mocked: Boolean(freshLoc.mocked),
                timestamp: format12HourTime(new Date()),
              };
              const stabilized = applyStationaryFilter(rawCoords);
              setLiveCoords(stabilized);
              lastHeartbeatSent.current = now;
              transmitHeartbeat(stabilized);
            } catch {
              if (anchorCoordsRef.current) {
                lastHeartbeatSent.current = now;
                const updated = { ...anchorCoordsRef.current, timestamp: format12HourTime(new Date()) };
                setLiveCoords(updated);
                transmitHeartbeat(updated);
              }
            }
          }
        }, 30000);

      } catch (err: any) {
        if (isSubscribed) {
          setGpsError(err?.message || 'Searching for GPS satellite signal...');
        }
      }
    };

    startLiveGpsTracking();

    return () => {
      isSubscribed = false;
      if (watcherSubscription) {
        watcherSubscription.remove();
      }
      if (heartbeatIntervalTimer) {
        clearInterval(heartbeatIntervalTimer);
      }
    };
  }, [currentUser?.username, currentUser?.id, applyStationaryFilter]);

  const campusCenter = useMemo(() => {
    const lat = geofenceConfig?.latitude ?? schoolProfile?.latitude ?? null;
    const lng = geofenceConfig?.longitude ?? schoolProfile?.longitude ?? null;
    const radius = geofenceConfig?.radius_meters ?? schoolProfile?.geofence_radius_meters ?? null;
    return { lat, lng, radius };
  }, [geofenceConfig, schoolProfile]);

  const liveDistanceMeters = useMemo(() => {
    if (!liveCoords || campusCenter.lat === null || campusCenter.lng === null) {
      return null;
    }
    return calculateDistanceMeters(
      liveCoords.latitude,
      liveCoords.longitude,
      campusCenter.lat,
      campusCenter.lng
    );
  }, [liveCoords, campusCenter]);

  const isInsideCampusPerimeter = useMemo(() => {
    if (liveDistanceMeters === null || campusCenter.radius === null) return null;
    return liveDistanceMeters <= campusCenter.radius;
  }, [liveDistanceMeters, campusCenter.radius]);

  const todayStr = getLocalTodayStr();
  const todayDtrRow: DtrDayRow | null =
    dtrData?.rows?.find((r) => r.date_str === todayStr) || null;

  const hasTappedInToday = Boolean(todayDtrRow?.am_arrival || todayDtrRow?.pm_arrival);
  const hasTappedOutToday = Boolean(
    (todayDtrRow?.pm_departure && todayDtrRow.pm_departure !== '') ||
      (todayDtrRow?.am_departure && !todayDtrRow?.pm_arrival)
  );

  const isOfficiallyOnDuty = hasTappedInToday && !hasTappedOutToday;

  const dutyStatusLabel = hasTappedInToday
    ? hasTappedOutToday
      ? 'DONE FOR TODAY'
      : 'ACTIVE ON DUTY'
    : 'NO CHECK-IN';

  const dutyStatusColor = hasTappedInToday
    ? hasTappedOutToday
      ? '#d97706'
      : '#059669'
    : '#64748b';

  const dutyStatusBg = hasTappedInToday
    ? hasTappedOutToday
      ? '#fef3c7'
      : '#ecfdf5'
    : '#f1f5f9';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isInsideCampusPerimeter === false && isOfficiallyOnDuty && campusCenter.radius !== null) {
      const accuracyMargin = liveCoords?.accuracy || 0;
      const excessDistance = (liveDistanceMeters || 0) - campusCenter.radius;

      if (excessDistance <= accuracyMargin) {
        return;
      }

      const now = Date.now();
      if (!advisoryAcknowledged || now - lastAdvisoryTimestamp.current > 300000) {
        setShowAdvisoryModal(true);
        lastAdvisoryTimestamp.current = now;
        Vibration.vibrate([0, 150, 100, 150]);
      }
    } else if (isInsideCampusPerimeter === true) {
      setShowAdvisoryModal(false);
      setAdvisoryAcknowledged(false);
    }
  }, [isInsideCampusPerimeter, isOfficiallyOnDuty, liveDistanceMeters, liveCoords, campusCenter.radius, advisoryAcknowledged]);

  const handleDismissAdvisory = () => {
    setShowAdvisoryModal(false);
    setAdvisoryAcknowledged(true);
  };

  const loadCachedData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [cachedSchool, cachedSections, cachedDtr, cachedGeo, activeBase] = await Promise.all([
        AsyncStorage.getItem('attendsure_cached_school'),
        AsyncStorage.getItem('attendsure_cached_sections'),
        AsyncStorage.getItem('attendsure_cached_dtr'),
        AsyncStorage.getItem('attendsure_cached_geofence'),
        getServerBaseUrl(),
      ]);

      if (cachedSchool) setSchoolProfile(JSON.parse(cachedSchool));
      if (cachedSections) setSections(JSON.parse(cachedSections));
      if (cachedDtr) setDtrData(JSON.parse(cachedDtr));
      if (cachedGeo) setGeofenceConfig(JSON.parse(cachedGeo));
      if (activeBase) setServerBase(activeBase);
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  const normalizeClasses = (rawList: any[]): SectionItem[] => {
    return rawList.map((item: any, idx: number) => ({
      schedule_id: item.schedule_id || item.id || idx + 1,
      section_id: item.section_id || item.section?.id || item.id || 0,
      section_name: item.section_name || item.section?.name || item.name || '',
      subject_name: item.subject_name || item.subject?.name || item.subject_title || '',
      subject_code: item.subject_code || item.subject?.code || '',
      room: item.room || item.room_number || item.room?.name || '',
      start_time: typeof item.start_time === 'string' ? item.start_time : '',
      end_time: typeof item.end_time === 'string' ? item.end_time : '',
      total_students: Array.isArray(item.students) ? item.students.length : Number(item.total_students) || 0,
      students: Array.isArray(item.students) ? item.students : [],
    }));
  };

  const flushOfflineRecords = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setIsSyncingQueue(true);
      const res = await syncOfflineQueue();
      const updatedCount = await getUnsyncedCount();
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setOfflineCount(updatedCount);
        if (res.synced > 0) {
          setIsOnline(true);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.log('[SyncQueue] Error:', err);
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setIsSyncingQueue(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadAllDynamicData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    const todayDate = getLocalTodayStr();
    const now = new Date();

    const [
      classListRes,
      schoolRes,
      dtrRes,
      geofenceRes,
      calendarRes,
    ] = await Promise.allSettled([
      apiClient.get('classroom/class_list/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 })
        .catch(() => apiClient.get('classroom/student_list/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 }))
        .catch(() => apiClient.get('sections/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 })),
      apiClient.get('settings/school/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 }),
      apiClient.get(`reports/dtr/?month=${now.getMonth() + 1}&year=${now.getFullYear()}`, { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 }),
      apiClient.get('geofence/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 }),
      apiClient.get('calendar/exceptions/', { signal: controller.signal, timeout: 5000 }),
    ]);

    if (!isMounted.current) return;

    if (classListRes.status === 'fulfilled' && classListRes.value?.data) {
      const rawPayload = classListRes.value.data;
      const rawList = Array.isArray(rawPayload)
        ? rawPayload
        : Array.isArray(rawPayload?.results)
        ? rawPayload.results
        : [];
      const normalized = normalizeClasses(rawList);
      setSections(normalized);
      AsyncStorage.setItem('attendsure_cached_sections', JSON.stringify(normalized)).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (schoolRes.status === 'fulfilled' && schoolRes.value?.data) {
      setSchoolProfile(schoolRes.value.data);
      AsyncStorage.setItem('attendsure_cached_school', JSON.stringify(schoolRes.value.data)).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (dtrRes.status === 'fulfilled' && dtrRes.value?.data) {
      setDtrData(dtrRes.value.data);
      AsyncStorage.setItem('attendsure_cached_dtr', JSON.stringify(dtrRes.value.data)).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (geofenceRes.status === 'fulfilled' && geofenceRes.value?.data) {
      setGeofenceConfig(geofenceRes.value.data);
      AsyncStorage.setItem('attendsure_cached_geofence', JSON.stringify(geofenceRes.value.data)).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (calendarRes.status === 'fulfilled' && calendarRes.value?.data) {
      const calPayload = calendarRes.value.data;
      const list: CalendarOverrideItem[] = Array.isArray(calPayload)
        ? calPayload
        : Array.isArray(calPayload?.results)
        ? calPayload.results
        : [];
      if (typeof calPayload?.weekend_rest_enabled === 'boolean') {
        setWeekendRestEnabled(calPayload.weekend_rest_enabled);
      }
      const matched = list.find((item) => item.date === todayDate) || null;
      setTodayOverride(matched);
    }

    const count = await getUnsyncedCount();
    setOfflineCount(count);
    if (count > 0) {
      flushOfflineRecords();
    }

    setLastSyncTime(format12HourTime(new Date()));
    setLoading(false);
  }, [flushOfflineRecords]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.all([loadAllDynamicData(), flushOfflineRecords(), refreshUser()]);
    if (isMounted.current) setRefreshing(false);
  }, [loadAllDynamicData, flushOfflineRecords, refreshUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    loadCachedData().then(() => {
      loadAllDynamicData();
    });
    refreshUser();

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) {
        abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      }
    };
  }, [loadCachedData, loadAllDynamicData, refreshUser]);

  const safeSections = Array.isArray(sections) ? sections : [];
  const totalClasses = safeSections.length;

  let totalStudentsEnrolled = 0;
  let totalPresent = 0;
  let totalLate = 0;
  let totalAbsent = 0;
  let totalNeedsReview = 0;
  let totalUnscanned = 0;

  let totalMaleEnrolled = 0;
  let totalFemaleEnrolled = 0;
  let totalMalePresent = 0;
  let totalFemalePresent = 0;

  safeSections.forEach((sec) => {
    const students = Array.isArray(sec?.students) ? sec.students : [];
    totalStudentsEnrolled += students.length;

    students.forEach((st) => {
      const status = String(st?.attendance_status || 'UNSCANNED').toUpperCase();
      const gender = String(st?.gender || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const isMale = gender.startsWith('M');
      const isFemale = gender.startsWith('F');

      if (isMale) totalMaleEnrolled += 1;
      if (isFemale) totalFemaleEnrolled += 1;

      if (status === 'PRESENT') {
        totalPresent += 1;
        if (isMale) totalMalePresent += 1;
        if (isFemale) totalFemalePresent += 1;
      } else if (status === 'LATE' || status === 'TARDY') {
        totalLate += 1;
        if (isMale) totalMalePresent += 1;
        if (isFemale) totalFemalePresent += 1;
      } else if (status === 'ABSENT') {
        totalAbsent += 1;
      } else if (status === 'CUTTING' || status === 'UNVERIFIED') {
        totalNeedsReview += 1;
      } else {
        totalUnscanned += 1;
      }
    });
  });

  const totalAttended = totalPresent + totalLate;
  const overallRate =
    totalStudentsEnrolled > 0
      ? Math.round((totalAttended / totalStudentsEnrolled) * 100)
      : 0;

  const isWeekendToday = [0, 6].includes(new Date().getDay());

  const rawLogoPath = schoolProfile?.school_logo || schoolProfile?.left_logo;
  const resolvedLogoUri = resolvePhotoUrl(rawLogoPath, serverBase);
  const logoSource = (resolvedLogoUri && !logoFailed) ? { uri: resolvedLogoUri } : fallbackLogo;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeContainer} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      <StatusBar style="dark" />

      {/* Campus Boundary Advisory Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={showAdvisoryModal}
        transparent={true}
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={handleDismissAdvisory}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalIconWrap}>
              <Ionicons name="location-outline" size={32} color="#004b87" />
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.modalTitle}>Outside School Area</Text>
            <Text style={styles.modalSubtitle}>
              You are signed in on duty, but your phone is currently outside the school boundary.
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.modalMetricsBox}>
              <View style={styles.modalMetricCol}>
                <Text style={styles.modalMetricLabel}>CURRENT DISTANCE</Text>
                <Text style={styles.modalMetricVal}>
                  {liveDistanceMeters !== null ? formatDistance(liveDistanceMeters) : '—'}
                </Text>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.modalMetricDivider} />
              <View style={styles.modalMetricCol}>
                <Text style={styles.modalMetricLabel}>ALLOWED BOUNDARY</Text>
                <Text style={styles.modalMetricVal}>
                  {campusCenter.radius !== null ? `${campusCenter.radius}m` : '—'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <Text style={styles.modalExplanation}>
              If you are on official school business, notify your department head. Otherwise, please return to campus grounds to verify your attendance.
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.modalConfirmBtn}
              onPress={handleDismissAdvisory}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
            >
              <Ionicons name="checkmark-outline" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
              <Text style={styles.modalConfirmBtnText}>I Understand</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <View style={styles.brandGroup}>
          <Image
            source={logoSource}
            style={styles.schoolLogo}
            contentFit="contain"
            onError={() => setLogoFailed(true)}
          />
          <View style={styles.brandTextGroup}>
            <Text style={styles.schoolTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              {schoolName || 'AttendSure'}
            </Text>
            {schoolSubHeader ? (
              <View style={styles.subBrandRow}>
                <View style={[styles.activeDot, { backgroundColor: isOnline ? '#16a34a' : '#94a3b8' }]} />
                <Text style={styles.protocolText} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {schoolSubHeader}
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.refreshButton}
          onPress={onRefresh}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          accessibilityLabel="Refresh records"
        >
          <Ionicons name="sync-outline" size={16} color="#004b87" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        style={styles.scrollArea}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Fake GPS Security Alert */}
        {liveCoords?.mocked && (
          <View style={styles.securityAlertCard}>
            <Ionicons name="alert-circle-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={styles.securityAlertTitle}>Fake GPS Detected</Text>
              <Text style={styles.securityAlertBody}>
                Mock location apps are running. Please turn off fake GPS in phone settings to keep official school logs valid.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        )}

        {/* 1. Pastel Welcome Banner Card */}
        <View style={styles.welcomeCard}>
          <View style={styles.welcomeHeader}>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.welcomeSub}>{mindfulQuote.greeting},</Text>
              <Text style={styles.welcomeTitle} numberOfLines={1}>{displayName}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.avatarBox}>
              {resolvedUserPhoto && !avatarFailed ? (
                <Image
                  source={{ uri: resolvedUserPhoto }}
                  style={styles.avatarImg}
                  contentFit="cover"
                  onError={() => setAvatarFailed(true)}
                />
              ) : (
                <Ionicons name="person" size={22} color={COLORS.primary} />
              )}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Status / Level Progress Bar */}
          <View style={styles.progressContainer}>
            <View style={styles.progressTopRow}>
              <Text style={styles.progressBadgeText}>{employeeId ? `ID: ${employeeId}` : 'Faculty'}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.progressPercent}>{overallRate}% Attendance Today</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.progressBarTrack}>
              <View style={[styles.progressBarFill, { width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(5, overallRate))}%` }]} />
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* 2. Primary Status / Classroom Summary Card */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.primaryStatusCard}
          onPress={() => onNavigate?.('class_list')}
          activeOpacity={0.9}
        >
          <View style={styles.statusTopRow}>
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={styles.statusCardEyebrow}>TODAY'S CLASS SUMMARY</Text>
              <Text style={styles.statusCardTitle}>
                {totalPresent} of {totalStudentsEnrolled} Learners Present
              </Text>
            </View>
            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="#ffffff" />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.statusFooterRow}>
            <View style={styles.statusPill}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>Needs Review: {totalNeedsReview}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.statusPill, { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' }]}>
              <Text style={styles.statusPillText}>GPS Verified: {isInsideCampusPerimeter ? 'On Campus' : 'Away'}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* 3. Quick Links Pastel Grid */}
        <Text style={styles.sectionHeader}>Quick Actions</Text>
        <View style={styles.gridContainer}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.gridCard, { backgroundColor: '#fff7ed' }]}
            onPress={() => onNavigate?.('scanner')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={[styles.gridIconBox, { backgroundColor: '#ffedd5' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="scan-outline" size={20} color="#c2410c" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.gridCardTitle, { color: '#9a3412' }]}>Face Check</Text>
            <Text style={styles.gridCardSub}>GPS &amp; Biometric</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.gridCard, { backgroundColor: '#eff6ff' }]}
            onPress={() => onNavigate?.('gate_pass')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={[styles.gridIconBox, { backgroundColor: '#dbeafe' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="ticket-outline" size={20} color="#1d4ed8" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.gridCardTitle, { color: '#1e40af' }]}>Exit Pass</Text>
            <Text style={styles.gridCardSub}>Gate Clearances</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.gridCard, { backgroundColor: '#f5f3ff' }]}
            onPress={() => onNavigate?.('class_list')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={[styles.gridIconBox, { backgroundColor: '#ede9fe' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="people-outline" size={20} color="#6d28d9" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.gridCardTitle, { color: '#5b21b6' }]}>Class List</Text>
            <Text style={styles.gridCardSub}>Enrolled Learners</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.gridCard, { backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5' }]}
            onPress={() => onNavigate?.('settings')}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={[styles.gridIconBox, { backgroundColor: '#d1fae5' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="notifications-outline" size={20} color="#047857" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.gridCardTitle, { color: '#065f46' }]}>Push Alerts</Text>
            <Text style={styles.gridCardSub}>Parent Broadcasts</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* 4. More Tools Expansion Card */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.moreToolsCard}
          onPress={() => onNavigate?.('menu')}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
        >
          <View style={styles.moreToolsLeft}>
            <View style={styles.moreToolsIconBox}>
              <Ionicons name="grid-outline" size={20} color={COLORS.primary} />
            </View>
            <View>
              <Text style={styles.moreToolsTitle}>More School Tools</Text>
              <Text style={styles.moreToolsSub}>DTR, Calendar, SARDO Risk Reports &amp; Settings</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color="#64748b" />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* School Campus Location Card */}
        <View style={styles.presenceShieldCard}>
          <View style={styles.presenceCardHeader}>
            <View style={styles.presenceHeaderLeft}>
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.pulseDot,
                  {
                    backgroundColor:
                      isInsideCampusPerimeter === true
                        ? '#16a34a'
                        : isInsideCampusPerimeter === false
                        ? '#d97706'
                        : '#94a3b8',
                  },
                ]}
              />
              <Text style={styles.presenceHeaderLabel}>SCHOOL CAMPUS LOCATION</Text>
            </View>

            {liveCoords?.timestamp ? (
              <View style={styles.timestampBadge}>
                <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={11} color="#64748b" />
                <Text style={styles.timestampBadgeText}>{liveCoords.timestamp}</Text>
              </View>
            ) : null}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.presenceMainContent}>
            {liveDistanceMeters !== null ? (
              <>
                <View style={styles.distanceValueRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.distanceMetricBig}>
                    {formatDistance(liveDistanceMeters)}
                  </Text>
                  <Text style={styles.distanceMetricSub}>from school grounds</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.perimeterBadgeRow}>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.perimeterBadge,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: isInsideCampusPerimeter ? '#ecfdf5' : '#fffbeb',
                        borderColor: isInsideCampusPerimeter ? '#a7f3d0' : '#fde68a',
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Ionicons
                      name={isInsideCampusPerimeter ? 'checkmark-circle' : 'navigate-circle-outline'}
                      size={13}
                      color={isInsideCampusPerimeter ? '#059669' : '#d97706'}
                    />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.perimeterBadgeText,
                        { color: isInsideCampusPerimeter ? '#047857' : '#b45309' },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {isInsideCampusPerimeter ? 'INSIDE SCHOOL GROUNDS' : 'OUTSIDE SCHOOL GROUNDS'}
                    </Text>
                  </View>

                  <Text style={styles.perimeterRadiusMeta}>
                    {campusCenter.radius !== null ? `Allowed area: ${campusCenter.radius}m` : 'Boundary unconfigured'}
                  </Text>
                </View>
              </>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.acquiringFixBox}>
                <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.acquiringFixText}>
                  {gpsError || 'Finding satellite location...'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Offline Buffer Banner */}
        {offlineCount > 0 && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.offlineBufferBanner}
            onPress={flushOfflineRecords}
            disabled={isSyncingQueue}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <View style={styles.offlineBannerLeft}>
              <Ionicons
                name={isSyncingQueue ? 'sync-outline' : 'cloud-upload-outline'}
                size={18}
                color="#b45309"
              />
              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                <Text style={styles.offlineBufferTitle}>
                  {offlineCount} {offlineCount === 1 ? 'record' : 'records'} saved on phone
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.offlineBufferSubtitle}>
                  {isSyncingQueue
                    ? 'Uploading to school server now...'
                    : isOnline
                    ? 'Tap here to upload now'
                    : 'Will upload when internet connects'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
            {isSyncingQueue ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#b45309" />
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.syncNowBtn}>
                <Text style={styles.syncNowBtnText}>Upload Now</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}

        {/* Calendar Holiday Banner */}
        {todayOverride ? (
          <View style={styles.calendarBanner}>
            <Ionicons name="calendar-outline" size={16} color="#004b87" />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={styles.calendarBannerTitle}>
                {todayOverride.type}: {todayOverride.date}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.calendarBannerReason}>{todayOverride.reason}</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        ) : isWeekendToday && weekendRestEnabled ? (
          <View style={[styles.calendarBanner, { backgroundColor: '#f0fdf4', borderColor: '#bbf7d0' }]}>
            <Ionicons name="partly-sunny-outline" size={16} color="#16a34a" />
            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
              <Text style={[styles.calendarBannerTitle, { color: '#15803d' }]}>
                Weekend Rest Day
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.calendarBannerReason, { color: '#166534' }]}>
                Duty logging is optional during scheduled rest days.
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* DTR Card */}
        {dtrData && (
          <>
            <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
              <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>
                Daily Time Record ({dtrData.month} {dtrData.year})
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderSub}>
                {dtrData.days_present} Days Logged
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.dtrIntegrityCard}>
              <View style={styles.dtrGrid}>
                <View style={styles.dtrSlot}>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotLabel}>AM IN</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotTime}>{todayDtrRow?.am_arrival || '--:--'}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.dtrSlot}>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotLabel}>AM OUT</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotTime}>{todayDtrRow?.am_departure || '--:--'}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.dtrSlot}>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotLabel}>PM IN</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotTime}>{todayDtrRow?.pm_arrival || '--:--'}</Text>
                </View>
                <View style={styles.dtrSlot}>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotLabel}>PM OUT</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.dtrSlotTime}>{todayDtrRow?.pm_departure || '--:--'}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.dtrCardFooter}>
                <Text style={styles.dtrFooterMeta}>
                  Undertime: {dtrData.total_undertime_hours}h {dtrData.total_undertime_minutes}m
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={[
                    styles.dtrApprovalBadge,
                    {
                      color: dtrData.dept_head_approved
                        ? '#16a34a'
                        : dtrData.dtr_submitted
                        ? '#004b87'
                        : '#64748b',
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  {dtrData.dept_head_approved
                    ? 'APPROVED BY DEPT HEAD'
                    : dtrData.dtr_submitted
                    ? 'SENT FOR REVIEW'
                    : 'LOGGING TODAY'}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          </>
        )}

        {/* Attendance Breakdown Tiles */}
        <View style={styles.sectionHeaderRow}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>Student Attendance Today</Text>
          {lastSyncTime ? (
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderSub}>Updated {lastSyncTime}</Text>
          ) : null}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.kpiTileGrid}>
          <View style={styles.kpiTile}>
            <View style={[styles.kpiIconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle-outline" size={15} color="#059669" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.kpiValueText, { color: '#059669' }]}>{totalPresent}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.kpiLabelText}>Present</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.kpiTile}>
            <View style={[styles.kpiIconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#fef3c7' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="time-outline" size={15} color="#d97706" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.kpiValueText, { color: '#d97706' }]}>{totalLate}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.kpiLabelText}>Late</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.kpiTile}>
            <View style={[styles.kpiIconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="close-circle-outline" size={15} color="#dc2626" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.kpiValueText, { color: '#dc2626' }]}>{totalAbsent}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.kpiLabelText}>Absent</Text>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.kpiTile}>
            <View style={[styles.kpiIconWrap, { backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' }]}>
              <Ionicons name="eye-outline" size={15} color="#475569" />
            </View>
            <Text style={[styles.kpiValueText, { color: '#334155' }]}>{totalNeedsReview}</Text>
            <Text style={styles.kpiLabelText}>Review</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'flex-start',
    backgroundColor: '#fef2f2',
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#fca5a5',
    borderRadius: 10,
    padding: 10,
    marginBottom: 12,
    gap: 8,
  },
  securityAlertTitle: { fontSize: 11.5, fontWeight: '800', color: '#991b1b' },

      ios: {
        shadowColor: COLORS.primaryDark,

      },
      android: { elevation: 6 },
    }),

    backgroundColor: '#0ea5e9',

  statusTopRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', marginBottom: 12 },

  statusCardTitle: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: '800', color: '#ffffff', marginTop: 2 },

  gridCard: { width: '48%', borderRadius: 16, padding: 14, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#e2e8f0', gap: 6 },

  moreToolsCard: {

    borderRadius: 16,

  },

  moreToolsIconBox: { width: 40, height: 40, borderRadius: 12, backgroundColor: '#eff6ff', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },

    alignItems: 'center',

================================================================================
# FILE: mobile\src\screens\DTRScreen.tsx

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\DTRScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Alert,
  Share,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';
import DTRReportView, { DTRReportData } from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

export const DTRScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const userRole = String(currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'FACULTY').trim().toUpperCase();
  const isFaculty = userRole === 'FACULTY';

  const now = new Date();
  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState<number>(now.getMonth() + 1);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<number>(now.getFullYear());
  const [selectedFacultyId, setSelectedFacultyId] = useState<string>('');

  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<DTRReportData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submittingDtr, setSubmittingDtr] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [exportingPdf, setExportingPdf] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Fetch DTR
  const fetchDTR = useCallback(
    async (silent = false) => {
      if (!silent && abortControllerRef.current) {
        abortControllerRef.current.abort();
      }
      const controller = new AbortController();
      if (!silent) abortControllerRef.current = controller;

      const endpoint =
        isFaculty && !selectedFacultyId
          ? '/reports/dtr/'
          : selectedFacultyId
          ? `/reports/dtr/${selectedFacultyId}/`
          : '/reports/dtr/';

      const cacheKey = `attendsure_dtr_${selectedFacultyId || 'self'}_${selectedMonth}_${selectedYear}`;

      if (!silent) {
        setLoading(true);
        try {
          const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
          if (cached && isMounted.current) {
            setDtrData(JSON.parse(cached));
          }
        } catch {
          // Continue to live fetch
        }
      }

      try {
        const res = await apiClient.get<DTRReportData>(endpoint, {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (!isMounted.current) return;

        setDtrData(res.data);
        await AsyncStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(res.data));
      } catch {
        // Keep cached state
      } finally {
        if (!silent && isMounted.current) {
          setLoading(false);
          setRefreshing(false);
        }
      }
    },
    [isFaculty, selectedFacultyId, selectedMonth, selectedYear]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    fetchDTR(false);

    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    };
  }, [fetchDTR]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchDTR(false);
  }, [fetchDTR]);

  // Certify and Submit DTR
  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;

    setSubmittingDtr(true);
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`/reports/dtr/${dtrData.faculty_id}/`, {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
        month: dtrData.month_number,
        year: dtrData.year,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours,
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours,
      });
      Alert.alert('Success', 'DTR submitted to your Department Head.');
      fetchDTR(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to submit DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setSubmittingDtr(false);
    }
  };

  // Export / Share
  const handleExport = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;

    if (!dtrData.dept_head_approved) {
      Alert.alert('Notice', 'Department Head endorsement required before exporting.');
      return;
    }

    setExportingPdf(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get('/reports/export/', {
        params: {
          report_type: 'DTR',
          faculty_id: dtrData.faculty_id,
          month: dtrData.month_number,
          year: dtrData.year,
        },
      });

      const title = res.data?.title || `CSC_Form_48_${dtrData.faculty_name}_${dtrData.month}_${dtrData.year}`;
      await Share.share({
        title,
        message: `${title}\nCivil Service Form No. 48 certified via AttendSure.`,
      });
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Notice', 'Could not export DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setExportingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeContainer} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topBar}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>DTR</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>Civil Service Form No. 48</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnSec, (loading || exportingPdf) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleExport}
            disabled={loading || exportingPdf || !dtrData?.dept_head_approved}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name={dtrData?.dept_head_approved ? 'share-outline' : 'lock-closed-outline'}
              size={15}
              color={dtrData?.dept_head_approved ? '#004b87' : '#94a3b8'}
            />
            <Text style={[styles.btnSecText, !dtrData?.dept_head_approved && { color: '#94a3b8' }]}>
              Export
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnPri, loading && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={() => fetchDTR(false)}
            disabled={loading}
          >
            {loading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <Ionicons name="refresh" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Month Selector */}
        <View style={styles.monthPillRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.navArrow}
            onPress={() => {
              if (selectedMonth === 1) {
                setSelectedMonth(12);
                setSelectedYear((y) => y - 1);
              } else {
                setSelectedMonth((m) => m - 1);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="chevron-back" size={16} color="#004b87" />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <Text style={styles.monthDisplay}>
            {MONTH_NAMES[selectedMonth - 1]} {selectedYear}
          </Text>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.navArrow}
            onPress={() => {
              if (selectedMonth === 12) {
                setSelectedMonth(1);
                setSelectedYear((y) => y + 1);
              } else {
                setSelectedMonth((m) => m + 1);
              }
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={16} color="#004b87" />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* DTR Component */}
        {loading && !dtrData ? (
          <View style={styles.loadingBox}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading records...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <DTRReportView
            data={dtrData}
            submitting={submittingDtr}
            onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
            onRefreshNeeded={() => fetchDTR(true)}
          />
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeContainer: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9' },
  topBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#cbd5e1',
  },
  screenTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '800', color: '#003460' },
  screenSub: { fontSize: 10, color: '#64748b' },
  btnPri: {
    backgroundColor: '#004b87',
    paddingVertical: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  btnSec: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    gap: 4,
    backgroundColor: '#e6f0fa',
    paddingVertical: 6,
    paddingHorizontal: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#b8d6f0',
  },
  btnSecText: { color: '#004b87', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: '800' },
  scrollContent: { padding: 12, paddingBottom: 110, gap: 10 },
  monthPillRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderRadius: 8,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#cbd5e1',
  },
  navArrow: { padding: 4 },
  monthDisplay: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: '800', color: '#0f172a' },
  loadingBox: { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', paddingVertical: 40 },
  loadingText: { fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b', marginTop: 6 },
});

export default DTRScreen;

# FILE: mobile\src\screens\GatePassScreen.tsx

/**
 * AttendSure - Mobile Gate Pass & Clearance Center
 * File: mobile/src/screens/GatePassScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  RefreshControl,
  Modal,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

export interface GatePassItem {
  id: number;
  pass_number: string;
  bearer_type: 'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY';
  student?: number | null;
  student_name?: string;
  student_lrn?: string;
  faculty?: number | null;
  faculty_name?: string;
  faculty_emp_id?: string;
  pass_type: string;
  reason: string;
  valid_from: string;
  valid_to: string;
  status: 'PENDING' | 'ACTIVE' | 'USED' | 'DECLINED' | 'EXPIRED';
  attachment_url?: string | null;
  adviser_name?: string;
}

interface GatePassScreenProps {
  onBack?: () => void;
}

export const GatePassScreen: React.FC<GatePassScreenProps> = ({ onBack }) => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userRole = String(user?.role || user?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE').toUpperCase();
  const isPrincipalOrAdmin = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL'].includes(userRole);
  const isRestrictedRole = ['PARENT', 'SECURITY'].includes(userRole);

  const [passes, setPasses] = useState<GatePassItem[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('ACTIVE');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const [selectedPass, setSelectedPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);
  const [showIssueModal, setShowIssueModal] = useState(false);
  const [passTarget, setPassTarget] = useState<'STUDENT' | 'FACULTY'>('STUDENT');
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const [reviewingPass, setReviewingPass] = useState<GatePassItem | null>(null);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    student_id: '',
    pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
    reason: '',
    duration_hours: '4',
  });

  const fetchPasses = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<any>('gate-passes/');
      const list = res.data?.results ?? (Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);
      setPasses(list);
    } catch {
      if (!silent) Alert.alert('Network Notice', 'Could not load gate passes.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPasses();
  }, [fetchPasses]);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    fetchPasses(true);
  };

  const filteredPasses = useMemo(() => {
    return passes.filter((p) => {
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'ALL' || p.status === statusFilter;
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      const passNum = (p.pass_number || '').toLowerCase();
      const bearer = (p.student_name || p.faculty_name || '').toLowerCase();
      const reason = (p.reason || '').toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch = !q || passNum.includes(q) || bearer.includes(q) || reason.includes(q);
      return matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
    });
  }, [passes, statusFilter, searchQuery]);

  const handleIssueSubmit = async () => {
    if (passTarget === 'STUDENT' && !form.student_id.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Required', 'Please enter the Student ID.');
      return;
    }

    if (!form.reason.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Required', 'Please state a valid reason or destination.');
      return;
    }

    const hours = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Number(form.duration_hours) || 4));
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + hours * 3600000);

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, any> = {
        bearer_type: passTarget,
        pass_type: form.pass_type,
        reason: form.reason.trim(),
        valid_from: now.toISOString(),
        valid_to: expiry.toISOString(),
      };

      if (passTarget === 'STUDENT') {
        payload.student = Number(form.student_id);
      }

      await apiClient.post('gate-passes/', payload);
      Alert.alert(
        'Success',
        passTarget === 'FACULTY'
          ? 'Your official travel pass was submitted to the Principal/Admin for document review.'
          : 'Student gate exit permit issued. School administration notified.'
      );

      setShowIssueModal(false);
      setForm({
        student_id: '',
        pass_type: 'Medical / Health Emergency',
        reason: '',
        duration_hours: '4',
      });
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert('Submission Error', err?.response?.data?.student?.[0] || 'Unable to submit gate pass.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleApprovePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/approve/`);
      Alert.alert('Approved', 'Travel pass approved and active for gate exit.');
      setReviewingPass(null);
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to approve pass.');
    }
  };

  const handleDeclinePass = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    Alert.alert('Decline Pass', 'Decline this travel pass due to an invalid or unauthentic document?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      {
        text: 'Confirm Decline',
        style: 'destructive',
        onPress: async () => {
          try {
            await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/decline/`, { reason: 'Declined upon document authenticity review.' });
            Alert.alert('Declined', 'Gate pass marked as DECLINED.');
            setReviewingPass(null);
            fetchPasses(true);
          } catch {
            Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to decline pass.');
          }
        },
      },
    ]);
  };

  const handleConfirmExit = async (pass: GatePassItem) => {
    try {
      await apiClient.post(`gate-passes/${pass.id}/mark-used/`);
      Alert.alert('Exit Logged', 'Bearer departure recorded. Pass marked as USED.');
      setSelectedPass(null);
      fetchPasses(true);
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Error', 'Failed to confirm exit.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      {/* Header with Back Arrow Button */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View style={styles.headerLeft}>
          {onBack && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.backBtn} onPress={onBack}>
              <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color="#004b87" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          <View>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Gate Clearances</Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSubtitle}>Student Permits &amp; Travel Orders</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        {!isRestrictedRole && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.newBtn}
            onPress={() => {
              setPassTarget('STUDENT');
              setShowIssueModal(true);
            }}
          >
            <Ionicons name="add" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
            <Text style={styles.newBtnText}>New Pass</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Search Input */}
      <View style={styles.searchBox}>
        <Ionicons name="search" size={16} color={COLORS.textMuted} />
        <TextInput
          style={styles.searchInput}
          placeholder="Search pass #, name, reason..."
          placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
        />
      </View>

      {/* Filter Row */}
      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
        <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false} contentContainerStyle={{ gap: 6 }}>
          {['ACTIVE', 'PENDING', 'ALL', 'USED', 'DECLINED'].map((st) => (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={st}
              style={[styles.filterChip, statusFilter === st && styles.filterChipActive]}
              onPress={() => setStatusFilter(st)}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.filterChipText, statusFilter === st && styles.filterChipTextActive]}>
                {st === 'PENDING' ? 'Pending Review' : st}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {/* Passes List */}
      {loading ? (
        <View style={styles.centerBox}>
          <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} />
          <Text style={styles.centerText}>Loading gate passes...</Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={filteredPasses}
          keyExtractor={(item) => String(item.id)}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
          refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />}
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const bearer = item.bearer_type === 'STUDENT' ? item.student_name : item.faculty_name;
            const isPending = item.status === 'PENDING';

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.card}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => (isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? setReviewingPass(item) : setSelectedPass(item))}
              >
                <View style={styles.cardTop}>
                  <View>
                    <Text style={styles.passCode}>{item.pass_number}</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.bearerType}>{item.bearer_type} CLEARANCE</Text>
                  </View>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.statusPill,
                      {
                        backgroundColor:
                          item.status === 'ACTIVE'
                            ? 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)'
                            : item.status === 'PENDING'
                            ? 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)'
                            : item.status === 'DECLINED'
                            ? 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12)'
                            : '#f1f5f9',
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.statusPillText,
                        {
                          color:
                            item.status === 'ACTIVE'
                              ? '#10b981'
                              : item.status === 'PENDING'
                              ? '#d97706'
                              : item.status === 'DECLINED'
                              ? '#ef4444'
                              : '#64748b',
                        },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {item.status}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.bearerName}>{bearer || 'Authorized Bearer'}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.reasonText} numberOfLines={2}>{item.reason}</Text>

                <View style={styles.cardBottom}>
                  <Text style={styles.dateText}>
                    Until: {new Date(item.valid_to).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
                  </Text>
                  {isPending && isPrincipalOrAdmin ? (
                    <Text style={[styles.cardAction, { color: '#d97706' }]}>Review Document &gt;</Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.cardAction}>View Slip &gt;</Text>
                  )}
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* MODAL 1: Create Pass with Proof Document */}
      <Modal visible={showIssueModal} transparent animationType="fade">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Create Gate Clearance</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowIssueModal(false)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
              <View style={styles.switchRow}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('STUDENT')}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'STUDENT' && styles.switchTextActive]}>
                    Student Exit (Adviser)
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={[styles.switchBtn, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchBtnActive]}
                  onPress={() => setPassTarget('FACULTY')}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.switchText, passTarget === 'FACULTY' && styles.switchTextActive]}>
                    Faculty Official Travel
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              {passTarget === 'STUDENT' ? (
                <>
                  <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Student Database ID *</Text>
                  <TextInput
                    style={styles.inputField}
                    placeholder="Enter Student ID"
                    keyboardType="numeric"
                    value={form.student_id}
                    onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, student_id: val })}
                  />
                </>
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.noticeText}>
                  Your travel pass will be submitted to the School Principal / Admin for document review.
                </Text>
              )}

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Valid Reason &amp; Destination *</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={[styles.inputField, { height: 60 }]}
                placeholder="State destination and purpose..."
                multiline
                value={form.reason}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, reason: val })}
              />

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Supporting Proof File *</Text>
              <View style={styles.proofBox}>
                <Ionicons name="document-attach-outline" size={18} color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.proofText}>Proof document selected (Required)</Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Allowed Duration (Hours)</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.inputField}
                placeholder="4"
                keyboardType="numeric"
                value={form.duration_hours}
                onChangeText={(val) => setForm({ ...form, duration_hours: val })}
              />

              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.submitBtn} onPress={handleIssueSubmit} disabled={submitting}>
                {submitting ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.submitBtnText}>Submit Gate Clearance</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </ScrollView>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 2: Principal / Admin Review */}
      <Modal visible={Boolean(reviewingPass)} transparent animationType="fade">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Inspect Proof Document</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setReviewingPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {reviewingPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <Text style={styles.detailTitle}>Applicant: {reviewingPass.faculty_name}</Text>
                <Text style={styles.detailDesc}>Reason: {reviewingPass.reason}</Text>

                <View style={styles.docBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.docBoxLabel}>ATTACHED PROOF DOCUMENT</Text>
                  {reviewingPass.attachment_url ? (
                    <Text style={styles.docBoxStatus}>Proof document attached on record.</Text>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={[styles.docBoxStatus, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>No attachment provided!</Text>
                  )}
                </View>

                <View style={styles.btnRow}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }]}
                    onPress={() => handleDeclinePass(reviewingPass)}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.actionBtnText, { color: '#ef4444' }]}>Decline (Unverified)</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: '#10b981' }]}
                    onPress={() => handleApprovePass(reviewingPass)}
                  >
                    <Text style={[styles.actionBtnText, { color: '#ffffff' }]}>Approve Pass</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* MODAL 3: Pass QR Slip */}
      <Modal visible={Boolean(selectedPass)} transparent animationType="slide">
        <View style={styles.modalBg}>
          <View style={styles.modalCard}>
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.modalHeading}>Gate Clearance Slip</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSelectedPass(null)}>
                <Ionicons name="close" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {selectedPass && (
              <ScrollView showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
                <View style={styles.qrArea}>
                  <Image
                    source={{
                      uri: `https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=180x180&data=${encodeURIComponent(
                        `ATTENDSURE-GATEPASS:${selectedPass.pass_number}`
                      )}`,
                    }}
                    style={styles.qrImage}
                    contentFit="contain"
                  />
                  <Text style={styles.qrHint}>Present to Gate shield upon exit.</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Pass Serial:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.pass_number}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Bearer:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>
                    {selectedPass.bearer_type === 'STUDENT' ? selectedPass.student_name : selectedPass.faculty_name}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Adviser / Issuer:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.adviser_name || 'Class Adviser'}</Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.infoRow}>
                  <Text style={styles.infoLabel}>Reason:</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.infoVal}>{selectedPass.reason}</Text>
                </View>

                {isRestrictedRole && selectedPass.status === 'ACTIVE' && (
                  <TouchableOpacity style={styles.confirmExitBtn} onPress={() => handleConfirmExit(selectedPass)}>
                    <Ionicons name="checkmark-circle" size={16} color="#ffffff" />
                    <Text style={styles.confirmExitBtnText}>Confirm Gate Exit</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: COLORS.background },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    backgroundColor: '#ffffff',
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: '#e2e8f0',
  },
  headerLeft: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 },
  backBtn: { width: 34, height: 34, borderRadius: 17, backgroundColor: '#f1f5f9', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
  headerTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: '800', color: '#003460' },

  newBtn: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: COLORS.primary, paddingVertical: 6, paddingHorizontal: 10, borderRadius: 8, gap: 4 },

  inputField: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: '#cbd5e1', borderRadius: 6, paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 6, fontSize: 12, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
