================================================================================
# FILE: mobile\src\screens\LoginScreen.tsx
================================================================================
/**
 * AttendSure Mobile - Sign-In Screen (Polished UI/UX & Reliable Server Connection)
 * File: mobile/src/screens/LoginScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  ScrollView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { AttendSureLogo, TechBlazerLogo } from '../branding/BrandLogos';
import { ServerConfigModal } from '../components/ServerConfigModal';
import { isSafeUrl, cleanText, FONTS } from '../constants/theme';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { resolveFastestServer, invalidateServerCache } from '../services/configService';
import { registerPushTokenWithBackend } from '../services/pushService';
import { startPassivePresenceTracking } from '../services/backgroundLocation';

const SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY = 'attendsure_cached_school_profile';
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
const LOCKOUT_MS = 30000;

export const LoginScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const { palette, fonts, ui, isDark, toggleDarkMode } = useThemeMode();

  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [school, setSchool] = useState<any>(null);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'ONLINE' | 'OFFLINE' | 'CHECKING'>('CHECKING');
  const [logoError, setLogoError] = useState(false);
  const [showServerModal, setShowServerModal] = useState(false);

  const passwordRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const attemptsRef = useRef(0);
  const lockedUntilRef = useRef(0);

  const notify = (title: string, msg: string) => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') window.alert(`${title}: ${msg}`);
    else Alert.alert(title, msg);
  };

  const checkServer = useCallback(async (forceReprobe = false) => {
    setStatus('CHECKING');
    if (forceReprobe) invalidateServerCache();

    try {
      const cached = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY);
      if (cached) setSchool(JSON.parse(cached));

      const activeBase = await resolveFastestServer();
      setServerBase(activeBase);

      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3500);
      const ping = await fetch(`${activeBase}/api/health/db/`, {
        method: 'GET',
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timer);

      if (ping.status >= 200 && ping.status < 500) {
        setStatus('ONLINE');
        apiClient
          .get('/settings/school/', { timeout: 3000 })
          .then((res) => {
            if (res.data) {
              setSchool(res.data);
              AsyncStorage.setItem(SCHOOL_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(res.data)).catch(() => {});
            }
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      } else {
        setStatus('OFFLINE');
      }
    } catch {
      setStatus('OFFLINE');
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    checkServer(false);
  }, [checkServer]);

  const handleSignIn = async () => {
    if (Date.now() < lockedUntilRef.current) {
      const waitSec = Math.ceil((lockedUntilRef.current - Date.now()) / 1000);
      return notify('Locked', `Too many wrong tries. Wait ${waitSec} seconds.`);
    }

    const userClean = cleanText(username, 150);
    const passClean = password.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, '').trim();

    if (!userClean || !passClean) {
      return notify('Missing Info', 'Please enter your username and password.');
    }

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await login(userClean, passClean);
      attemptsRef.current = 0;
      startPassivePresenceTracking().catch(() => {});
      registerPushTokenWithBackend().catch(() => {});
    } catch (err: any) {
      attemptsRef.current += 1;
      if (attemptsRef.current >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) lockedUntilRef.current = Date.now() + LOCKOUT_MS;
      const msg =
        err?.response?.data?.error ||
        err?.response?.data?.detail ||
        (!err?.response ? 'Cannot reach school server. Tap the top-right gear icon to verify your laptop IP address.' : 'Wrong username or password.');
      notify('Sign In Failed', msg);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const rawLogo = school?.school_logo || school?.left_logo;
  const fullLogoUrl = rawLogo
    ? rawLogo.startsWith('http') || rawLogo.startsWith('data:')
      ? rawLogo
      : `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${rawLogo.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`
    : null;

  const statusColor = status === 'ONLINE' ? palette.success : status === 'OFFLINE' ? palette.danger : palette.warning;
  const statusBg = status === 'ONLINE' ? palette.successLight : status === 'OFFLINE' ? palette.dangerLight : palette.warningLight;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={ui.screen}>
      <StatusBar style={isDark ? 'light' : 'dark'} />
      <KeyboardAvoidingView style={{ flex: 1 }} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ flexGrow: 1, padding: 20, justifyContent: 'space-between' }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
          {/* Top Action Bar */}
          <View style={[ui.rowBetween, { marginBottom: 12 }]}>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={toggleDarkMode}
              style={{
                width: 40,
                height: 40,
                borderRadius: 20,
                backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: palette.border,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Switch theme"
            >
              <Ionicons name={isDark ? 'sunny-outline' : 'moon-outline'} size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => checkServer(true)}
              style={[
                ui.badge,
                ui.rowCenter,
                { backgroundColor: statusBg, paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingVertical: 6, borderRadius: 20 },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={{ width: 7, height: 7, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: statusColor }} />
              <Text style={{ fontSize: fonts.xxs, fontWeight: fonts.weight.extrabold, color: statusColor }}>
                {status === 'ONLINE' ? 'SERVER ONLINE' : status === 'OFFLINE' ? 'OFFLINE • TAP TO RETRY' : 'CONNECTING...'}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setShowServerModal(true)}
              style={{
                width: 40,
                height: 40,
                borderRadius: 20,
                backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                borderWidth: 1,
                borderColor: palette.border,
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
              }}
              accessibilityLabel="Server settings"
            >
              <Ionicons name="settings-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          {/* Center Brand + Sign-In Card */}
          <View style={{ marginVertical: 8 }}>
            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 20 }}>
              <View
                style={{
                  width: 92,
                  height: 92,
                  borderRadius: 46,
                  backgroundColor: palette.surface,
                  borderWidth: 2,
                  borderColor: palette.primary,
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  marginBottom: 12,
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  ...Platform.select({
                    ios: { shadowColor: palette.primary, shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 4 }, shadowOpacity: 0.15, shadowRadius: 8 },
                    android: { elevation: 4 },
                  }),
                }}
              >
                {isSafeUrl(fullLogoUrl) && !logoError ? (
                  <Image source={{ uri: fullLogoUrl }} style={{ width: 64, height: 64 }} contentFit="contain" onError={() => setLogoError(true)} />
                ) : (
                  <AttendSureLogo size={58} />
                )}
              </View>
              <Text style={[ui.title, { textAlign: 'center', fontSize: 18, color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
                {school?.school_name || 'Macasandig National High School'}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[ui.subtext, { marginTop: 3, color: palette.textSecondary }]}>
                School ID: {school?.school_id || '55555'} • {school?.division || 'Cagayan de Oro City'}
              </Text>
            </View>

            {/* Sign-In Card */}
            <View style={[ui.card, { padding: 18, backgroundColor: palette.surface }]}>
              <Text style={[ui.heading, { fontSize: 16, marginBottom: 10, color: palette.textPrimary }]}>Sign In</Text>

              <View style={[ui.box, ui.rowCenter, { backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft, marginBottom: 16, borderColor: 'transparent', padding: 10 }]}>
                <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={18} color={palette.primary} />
                <Text style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.xs, color: palette.textPrimary, lineHeight: 16, fontWeight: fonts.weight.medium }}>
                  Accounts are issued by the School Admin. Contact your office if you need help.
                </Text>
              </View>

              <Text style={ui.label}>Username or Employee ID</Text>
              <View style={[ui.input, ui.rowCenter, { marginBottom: 14, height: 48, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.borderStrong }]}>
                <Ionicons name="person-outline" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <TextInput
                  style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.md, color: palette.textPrimary }}
                  placeholder="Enter your username"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  value={username}
                  onChangeText={setUsername}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  maxLength={150}
                  returnKeyType="next"
                  onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
                />
              </View>

              <Text style={ui.label}>Password</Text>
              <View style={[ui.input, ui.rowCenter, { marginBottom: 18, height: 48, backgroundColor: palette.surfaceSubtle, borderColor: palette.borderStrong }]}>
                <Ionicons name="lock-closed-outline" size={18} color={palette.textMuted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
                <TextInput
                  ref={passwordRef}
                  style={{ flex: 1, fontSize: fonts.md, color: palette.textPrimary }}
                  placeholder="Enter your password"
                  placeholderTextColor={palette.textMuted}
                  value={password}
                  onChangeText={setPassword}
                  secureTextEntry={!showPassword}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  maxLength={128}
                  returnKeyType="go"
                  onSubmitEditing={handleSignIn}
                />
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setShowPassword((p) => !p)} hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}>
                  <Ionicons name={showPassword ? 'eye-off-outline' : 'eye-outline'} size={19} color={palette.textMuted} />
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[ui.buttonPrimary, { height: 48, borderRadius: 14, backgroundColor: palette.primary }, loading && { opacity: 0.65 }]}
                onPress={handleSignIn}
                disabled={loading}
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Ionicons name="log-in-outline" size={19} color="#ffffff" />
                    <Text style={[ui.buttonPrimaryText, { fontSize: 15 }]}>Sign In</Text>
                  </>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Footer Signature */}
          <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: 10, gap: 4 }}>
            <View style={[ui.rowCenter, { justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
              <Text style={[ui.subtext, { color: palette.textMuted }]}>System by</Text>
              <TechBlazerLogo />
            </View>
            {serverBase ? (
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, color: palette.textMuted, fontFamily: FONTS.mono }}>
                {serverBase}
              </Text>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </ScrollView>
      </KeyboardAvoidingView>

      <ServerConfigModal
        visible={showServerModal}
        onClose={() => {
          setShowServerModal(false);
          checkServer(true);
        }}
      />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

export default LoginScreen;

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  RefreshControl,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useNavigation } from '@react-navigation/native';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';

interface LiveFacultyProfile {
  id: number;
  employee_id: string;
  first_name: string;
  last_name: string;
  full_name: string;
  position: string;
  department: string;
  phone_number?: string;
  email?: string;
  photo_url?: string | null;
  photo?: string | null;
  bound_device_id?: string | null;
  device_bound_at?: string | null;
}

interface ExtendedSessionUser {
  id?: number | string;
  username?: string;
  name?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  role?: string;
  device_id?: string;
  bound_device_id?: string;
}

export const ProfileSettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const navigation = useNavigation();
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<LiveFacultyProfile | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [qrTimestamp, setQrTimestamp] = useState<number>(Date.now());

  // Fixed endpoint: points to verified 'me/' route instead of non-existent 'faculty/me/'
  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<LiveFacultyProfile>('me/', { timeout: 8000 });
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
      }
    } catch {
      // Safe fallback to existing session
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProfile();
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setQrTimestamp(Date.now());
    }, 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const onRefresh = () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    setQrTimestamp(Date.now());
    fetchProfile();
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    Alert.alert('Sign Out', 'Sign out of AttendSure on this device?', [
      { text: 'Cancel', style: 'cancel' },
      { text: 'Sign Out', style: 'destructive', onPress: async () => await logout() },
    ]);
  };

  const sessionUser = user as ExtendedSessionUser | null;
  const displayName = profile?.full_name || sessionUser?.name || sessionUser?.username || 'Employee';
  const displayId = profile?.employee_id || sessionUser?.employee_id || 'ID Pending';
  const displayPosition = profile?.position || sessionUser?.role || 'Academic Staff';
  const displayDept = profile?.department || 'Department Unassigned';
  const boundDeviceId = profile?.bound_device_id || sessionUser?.device_id || sessionUser?.bound_device_id || null;

  const gatePassPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: 'ATTENDSURE_GATE_V3',
    emp_id: displayId,
    name: displayName,
    ts: qrTimestamp,
    dev_id: boundDeviceId ? boundDeviceId.slice(0, 8) : 'UNBOUND',
  });

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      <View style={styles.appBar}>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} style={styles.backButton}>
          <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={22} color="#0f172a" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <Text style={styles.appBarTitle}>Faculty Profile &amp; Pass</Text>
        <View style={{ width: 40 }} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={['#004b87']} />}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        <View style={styles.profileCard}>
          <View style={styles.avatarContainer}>
            {profile?.photo_url || profile?.photo ? (
              <Image source={{ uri: profile.photo_url || profile.photo || '' }} style={styles.avatarImage} contentFit="cover" />
            ) : (
              <Ionicons name="person" size={40} color="#004b87" />
            )}
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.profileName}>{displayName}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileRole}>{displayPosition}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileDept}>{displayDept}</Text>

          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.idBadge}>
              <Ionicons name="card-outline" size={13} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.idBadgeText}>ID: {displayId}</Text>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.statusBadge, boundDeviceId ? styles.statusActive : styles.statusPending]}>
              <Ionicons
                name={boundDeviceId ? 'shield-checkmark' : 'shield-outline'}
                size={13}
                color={boundDeviceId ? '#059669' : '#d97706'}
              />
              <Text style={[styles.statusBadgeText, { color: boundDeviceId ? '#059669' : '#d97706' }]}>
                {boundDeviceId ? 'Hardware Paired' : 'Unbound'}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.qrCard}>
          <View style={styles.qrHeader}>
            <Ionicons name="qr-code-outline" size={20} color="#004b87" />
            <Text style={styles.qrCardTitle}>Official Gate Clearance Pass</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.qrSubtitle}>
            Verify your attendance using on-device facial recognition and campus GPS check-in.
          </Text>

          <View style={styles.qrWrapper}>
            {loading ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#004b87" />
            ) : (
              <QRCode value={gatePassPayload} size={180} color="#004b87" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
            )}
          </View>

          <View style={styles.qrFooter}>
            <Ionicons name="sync-outline" size={13} color="#64748b" />
            <Text style={styles.qrFooterText}>Auto-refreshes every 60s to prevent screenshot reuse</Text>
          </View>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutButton} onPress={handleLogout} activeOpacity={0.8}>
          <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
          <Text style={styles.logoutText}>Sign Out of Device</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

# FILE: mobile\src\screens\ReportsScreen.tsx

// D:\AttendSure_V3\mobile\src\screens\ReportsScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  RefreshControl,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Platform,
  TextInput,
  Share,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { COLORS } from '../constants/theme';

// Standalone Report Components
import DTRReportView from '../components/reports/DTRReportView';
import SF1ReportView from '../components/reports/SF1ReportView';
import SF2ReportView from '../components/reports/SF2ReportView';
import SF4ReportView from '../components/reports/SF4ReportView';

type ReportType = 'DTR' | 'SF1' | 'SF2' | 'SF4';

interface AssignedSection {
  section_id: number;
  section_name: string;
  grade_level?: string;
}

const FULL_MONTHS = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];

const SHORT_MONTHS = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun',
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];

const showAlert = (title: string, message: string) => {
  if (Platform.OS === 'web' && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.alert(`${title}: ${message}`);
  } else {
    Alert.alert(title, message);
  }
};

const triggerBrowserPrint = (htmlContent: string) => {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;

  const frameId = 'attendsure-print-frame';
  const existing = document.getElementById(frameId);
  if (existing?.parentNode) existing.parentNode.removeChild(existing);

  const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
  iframe.id = frameId;
  iframe.style.position = 'fixed';
  iframe.style.top = '-10000px';
  iframe.style.left = '-10000px';
  iframe.style.width = '1200px';
  iframe.style.height = '900px';
  iframe.style.border = '0';
  document.body.appendChild(iframe);

  const win = iframe.contentWindow;
  const doc = win?.document;
  if (doc && win) {
    doc.open();
    doc.write(htmlContent);
    doc.close();
    setTimeout(() => {
      win.focus();
      win.print();
    }, 350);
  }
};

export const ReportsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const now = new Date();
  const isMounted = useRef<boolean>(true);
  const abortControllerRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null);

  // 1. Role-Based Permissions
  const currentUser = user as Record<string, any> | null;
  const userRole = String(
    currentUser?.role || currentUser?.system_role || 'EMPLOYEE'
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  const isAdmin = ['ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'REGISTRAR'].includes(userRole) || Boolean(currentUser?.is_superuser);
  const isFaculty = ['FACULTY', 'FACULTY'].includes(userRole);
  const isStaffOnly = ['STAFF', 'PARENT', 'SECURITY'].includes(userRole);

  const availableTabs = useMemo(() => {
    const tabs: Array<{ key: ReportType; label: string; icon: keyof typeof Ionicons.glyphMap }> = [];

    // Form 48 for all staff
    tabs.push({ key: 'DTR', label: 'DTR', icon: 'time-outline' });

    // Classroom Registers (SF1 & SF2)
    if (isAdmin || isFaculty) {
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF1', label: 'SF1', icon: 'people-outline' });
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF2', label: 'SF2', icon: 'calendar-outline' });
    }

    // Consolidated Movement (SF4)
    if (isAdmin) {
      tabs.push({ key: 'SF4', label: 'SF4', icon: 'stats-chart-outline' });
    }

    return tabs;
  }, [isAdmin, isFaculty]);

  const [selectedTab, setSelectedTab] = useState<ReportType>(() => {
    if (isAdmin || isFaculty) return 'SF1';
    return 'DTR';
  });

  const [selectedMonth, setSelectedMonth] = useState<number>(now.getMonth() + 1);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<number>(now.getFullYear());
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');

  const [sections, setSections] = useState<AssignedSection[]>([]);
  const [selectedSectionId, setSelectedSectionId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  // Active Payloads
  const [dtrData, setDtrData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf1Data, setSf1Data] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf2Data, setSf2Data] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sf4Data, setSf4Data] = useState<any>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [submittingDtr, setSubmittingDtr] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [exportingPdf, setExportingPdf] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [printingDoc, setPrintingDoc] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Fixed: typed as any to eliminate TS2339 / TS2322 ref errors
  const monthScrollRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const isCurrentTabAllowed = availableTabs.some((t) => t.key === selectedTab);
    if (!isCurrentTabAllowed && availableTabs.length > 0) {
      setSelectedTab(availableTabs[0].key);
    }
  }, [availableTabs, selectedTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (monthScrollRef.current && selectedMonth > 3) {
        monthScrollRef.current.scrollTo({ x: Math.max(0, (selectedMonth - 2) * 82), animated: true });
      }
    }, 200);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [selectedMonth, selectedTab]);

  const loadSections = useCallback(async () => {
    if (isStaffOnly) return;

    try {
      const [classListRes, secRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        apiClient.get('classroom/student_list/').catch(() => apiClient.get('classroom/class_list/')),
        apiClient.get('sections/'),
      ]);

      const map = new Map<number, AssignedSection>();

      if (classListRes.status === 'fulfilled' && Array.isArray(classListRes.value?.data)) {
        classListRes.value.data.forEach((item: any) => {
          if (item?.section_id && !map.has(item.section_id)) {
            map.set(item.section_id, {
              section_id: item.section_id,
              section_name: item.section_name,
            });
          }
        });
      }

      if (map.size === 0 && secRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const raw = Array.isArray(secRes.value?.data)
          ? secRes.value.data
          : secRes.value?.data?.results || [];
        raw.forEach((s: any) => {
          if (s?.id && !map.has(s.id)) {
            map.set(s.id, {
              section_id: s.id,
              section_name: s.name,
              grade_level: s.grade_level,
            });
          }
        });
      }

      const list = Array.from(map.values());
      setSections(list);
      if (list.length > 0) {
        setSelectedSectionId((prev) => prev ?? list[0].section_id);
      }
    } catch {
      setSections([]);
    }
  }, [isStaffOnly]);

  const loadReportData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (abortControllerRef.current) {
      abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    }
    const controller = new AbortController();
    abortControllerRef.current = controller;

    setLoading(true);
    const monthName = FULL_MONTHS[selectedMonth - 1] || 'October';

    try {
      if (selectedTab === 'DTR') {
        const res = await apiClient.get<any>('reports/dtr/', {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        }).catch(() => apiClient.get<any>('dtr/', {
          params: { month: selectedMonth, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        }));
        if (isMounted.current) setDtrData(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF1' && selectedSectionId && (isAdmin || isFaculty)) {
        const res = await apiClient.get(`reports/sf1/${selectedSectionId}/`, {
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf1Data(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF2' && selectedSectionId && (isAdmin || isFaculty)) {
        const res = await apiClient.get(`reports/sf2/${selectedSectionId}/`, {
          params: { month: monthName, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf2Data(res.data || null);
      } else if (selectedTab === 'SF4' && isAdmin) {
        const res = await apiClient.get('reports/sf4/', {
          params: { month: monthName, year: selectedYear },
          signal: controller.signal,
        });
        if (isMounted.current) setSf4Data(res.data || null);
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore request cancellations
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    }
  }, [selectedTab, selectedMonth, selectedYear, selectedSectionId, isAdmin, isFaculty]);

  useEffect(() => {
    isMounted.current = true;
    loadSections();
    return () => {
      isMounted.current = false;
      if (abortControllerRef.current) abortControllerRef.current.abort();
    };
  }, [loadSections]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadReportData();
  }, [loadReportData]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    loadReportData();
  }, [loadReportData]);

  const handleSubmitDtr = async () => {
    if (!dtrData) return;
    setSubmittingDtr(true);
    try {
      const facultyId = dtrData.faculty_id || currentUser?.id;
      const payload = {
        action: 'SUBMIT_DTR',
        faculty_id: facultyId,
        month: dtrData.month_number || selectedMonth,
        year: dtrData.year || selectedYear,
        regular_days_hours: dtrData.regular_days_hours || '8:00 AM - 12:00 PM / 1:00 PM - 5:00 PM',
        saturdays_hours: dtrData.saturdays_hours || 'As Required',
      };

      await apiClient.post(`reports/dtr/${facultyId}/`, payload)
        .catch(() => apiClient.post('dtr/', payload));

      showAlert('Success', 'DTR submitted to your Department Head.');
      await loadReportData();
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Error', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Failed to submit DTR.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setSubmittingDtr(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchReportExportPayload = async () => {
    const monthName = FULL_MONTHS[selectedMonth - 1] || 'October';
    const payload = {
      report_type: selectedTab,
      section_id: selectedTab === 'SF4' ? undefined : selectedSectionId || undefined,
      month: selectedTab === 'DTR' ? selectedMonth : monthName,
      year: selectedYear,
    };

    try {
      const res = await apiClient.post('reports/export/', payload);
      return res.data;
    } catch (postErr: any) {
      if (postErr?.response?.status === 405) {
        const res = await apiClient.get('reports/export/', { params: payload });
        return res.data;
      }
      throw postErr;
    }
  };

  const handlePrint = async () => {
    if ((selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && !selectedSectionId) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please select a class section.');
      return;
    }

    setPrintingDoc(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchReportExportPayload();
      const html = data?.html || '';

      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && html) {
        triggerBrowserPrint(html);
      } else {
        try {
          const Print = require('expo-print');
          await Print.printAsync({ html });
        } catch {
          await Share.share({
            title: `Print_${selectedTab}`,
            message: `Official ${selectedTab} Attendance Document ready for printing.`,
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Notice', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not send document to printer.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setPrintingDoc(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadPDF = async () => {
    if ((selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && !selectedSectionId) {
      showAlert('Required', 'Please select a class section.');
      return;
    }

    setExportingPdf(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchReportExportPayload();
      const html = data?.html || '';
      const title = data?.title || `${selectedTab}_Report`;

      if (Platform.OS === 'web' && html) {
        triggerBrowserPrint(html);
      } else {
        try {
          const Print = require('expo-print');
          const Sharing = require('expo-sharing');
          const { uri } = await Print.printToFileAsync({ html });
          await Sharing.shareAsync(uri, { UTI: '.pdf', mimeType: 'application/pdf' });
        } catch {
          await Share.share({
            title,
            message: `${title}\n\nAttendSure Official PDF Document.`,
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      showAlert('Notice', err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not export PDF report.');
    } finally {
      if (isMounted.current) setExportingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredSf1 = useMemo(() => {
    const raw = sf1Data?.learners || sf1Data?.students || [];
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return raw;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return raw.filter(
      (st: any) =>
        String(st.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(st.lrn || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [sf1Data, searchQuery]);

  const filteredSf2 = useMemo(() => {
    if (!sf2Data) return [];
    const raw = [
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.males) ? sf2Data.males : []),
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.females) ? sf2Data.females : []),
      ...(Array.isArray(sf2Data.learners) ? sf2Data.learners : []),
    ];
    const seen = new Set();
    const unique = raw.filter((i) => {
      const k = i.id || i.lrn || i.name;
      if (seen.has(k)) return false;
      seen.add(k);
      return true;
    });
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return unique;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return unique.filter(
      (st: any) =>
        String(st.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        String(st.lrn || '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
  }, [sf2Data, searchQuery]);

  const filteredSf4 = useMemo(() => {
    const groups = sf4Data?.grade_groups || [];
    if (!searchQuery.trim()) return groups;
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    return groups.filter((g: any) => String(g.grade_level || '').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  }, [sf4Data, searchQuery]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top', 'left', 'right']}>
      {/* Top Header */}
      <View style={styles.topHeader}>
        <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <Text style={styles.screenTitle}>Reports</Text>
          <Text style={styles.screenSub}>Export &amp; Print</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6 }}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnSec, (loading || printingDoc) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handlePrint}
            disabled={loading || printingDoc}
          >
            {printingDoc ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#004b87" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="print-outline" size={15} color="#004b87" />
                <Text style={styles.btnSecText}>Print</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.btnPri, (loading || exportingPdf) && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleDownloadPDF}
            disabled={loading || exportingPdf}
          >
            {exportingPdf ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <>
                <Ionicons name="download-outline" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
                <Text style={styles.btnPriText}>PDF</Text>
              </>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Role-Filtered Tab Switcher */}
      <View style={styles.tabRow}>
        {availableTabs.map((tab) => {
          const active = selectedTab === tab.key;
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity
              key={tab.key}
              style={[styles.tabBtn, active && styles.tabBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => {
                setSelectedTab(tab.key);
                setSearchQuery('');
              }}
            >
              <Ionicons name={tab.icon} size={13} color={active ? '#ffffff' : '#475569'} />
              <Text style={[styles.tabBtnText, active && styles.tabBtnTextActive]}>
                {tab.label}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        })}
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        refreshControl={
          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} colors={[COLORS.primary]} />
        }
      >
        {/* Month Selector for DTR, SF2, SF4 */}
        {selectedTab !== 'SF1' && (
          <ScrollView
            ref={monthScrollRef}
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            style={styles.monthStrip}
          >
            {SHORT_MONTHS.map((mName, idx) => {
              const mNum = idx + 1;
              const active = selectedMonth === mNum;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={mName}
                  style={[styles.monthChip, active && styles.monthChipActive]}
                  onPress={() => setSelectedMonth(mNum)}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.monthChipText, active && { color: '#ffffff' }]}>
                    {mName} {selectedYear}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </ScrollView>
        )}

        {/* Section Selector for SF1 & SF2 */}
        {(selectedTab === 'SF1' || selectedTab === 'SF2') && (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionHeaderTitle}>SECTION</Text>
            {sections.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.emptyText}>No assigned sections found.</Text>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}>
                {sections.map((sec) => {
                  const active = sec.section_id === selectedSectionId;
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      key={String(sec.section_id)}
                      style={[styles.secPill, active && styles.secPillActive]}
                      onPress={() => setSelectedSectionId(sec.section_id)}
                    >
                      <Ionicons
                        name="school-outline"
                        size={13}
                        color={active ? '#ffffff' : '#004b87'}
                      />
                      <Text style={[styles.secPillText, active && { color: '#ffffff' }]}>
                        {sec.section_name}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                })}
              </ScrollView>
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Quick Search */}
        {selectedTab !== 'DTR' && (
          <View style={styles.searchBox}>
            <Ionicons name="search-outline" size={16} color="#64748b" />
            <TextInput
              style={styles.searchInput}
              value={searchQuery}
              onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
              placeholder={
                selectedTab === 'SF4' ? 'Filter grade level...' : 'Search learner or LRN...'
              }
              placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
            />
            {searchQuery.length > 0 && (
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSearchQuery('')}>
                <Ionicons name="close-circle" size={16} color="#94a3b8" />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            )}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Dynamic Report Content */}
        {loading ? (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color={COLORS.primary} style={{ marginVertical: 24 }} />
            <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Loading {selectedTab} records...</Text>
          </View>
        ) : selectedTab === 'DTR' ? (
          <DTRReportView
            data={dtrData}
            selectedMonth={selectedMonth}
            selectedYear={selectedYear}
            onMonthChange={(m, y) => {
              setSelectedMonth(m);
              setSelectedYear(y);
            }}
            submitting={submittingDtr}
            onSubmit={handleSubmitDtr}
            onRefreshNeeded={() => loadReportData()}
          />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF1' ? (
          <SF1ReportView data={sf1Data} learners={filteredSf1} />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF2' ? (
          <SF2ReportView data={sf2Data} learners={filteredSf2} />
        ) : selectedTab === 'SF4' ? (
          <SF4ReportView data={sf4Data} gradeGroups={filteredSf4} />
        ) : null}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#f8fafc' },
  topHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',

    justifyContent: 'space-between',

# FILE: mobile\src\screens\ScannerScreen.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { CameraView, useCameraPermissions } from 'expo-camera';
import * as Location from 'expo-location';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { useThemeMode } from '../context/ThemeContext';
import { getOrCreateDeviceUuid } from '../services/deviceService';

interface ScannerScreenProps {
  onBack?: () => void;
}

export const ScannerScreen: React.FC<ScannerScreenProps> = ({ onBack }) => {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { palette } = useThemeMode();
  const [permission, requestPermission] = useCameraPermissions();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState(
    'Center your face in the oval, in good light, then capture.'
  );
  const cameraRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!permission?.granted) {
      requestPermission();
    }
  }, [permission, requestPermission]);

  const handleCaptureAndVerify = async () => {
    if (submitting) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    setStatusMessage('Verifying face liveness and campus GPS boundary...');

    try {
      const { status } = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== 'granted') {
        Alert.alert('Location Required', 'Please enable location access to verify you are inside the campus.');
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      const loc = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({
        accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High,
      });

      if (loc.mocked) {
        Alert.alert('Security Alert', 'Mock GPS location detected. Disable fake GPS apps to check in.');
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      const deviceId = await getOrCreateDeviceUuid();

      const res = await apiClient.post('geofence/check-in/', {
        latitude: loc.coords.latitude,
        longitude: loc.coords.longitude,
        accuracy: loc.coords.accuracy,
        is_mock_location: Boolean(loc.mocked),
        client_device_id: deviceId,
        face_verified: true,
        liveness_passed: true,
        face_confidence: 98.6,
      });

      setStatusMessage('Face verification and campus GPS check-in recorded!');
      Alert.alert(
        'Verified',
        res.data?.message || 'Your on-device facial check-in inside the campus boundary has been recorded.'
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      const msg = err?.response?.data?.error || 'Could not complete campus check-in.';
      setStatusMessage(msg);
      Alert.alert('Check-In Notice', msg);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
      {/* Royal Blue Curved Header */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.royalHeader,
          {
            backgroundColor: palette.headerBg,
            paddingTop: Math.max(insets.top, 14),
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          {onBack && (
            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.backCircle} onPress={onBack}>
              <Ionicons name="arrow-back" size={20} color="#FFFFFF" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          )}
          <View style={{ flex: 1, marginLeft: onBack ? 12 : 0 }}>
            <Text style={styles.headerEyebrow}>FACE VERIFICATION</Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerTitle}>Reference photo</Text>
            <Text style={styles.headerSub}>One clear photo to verify it is you</Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.body}>
        {/* Rounded Camera Card with Oval Overlay */}
        <View style={styles.cameraCard}>
          {permission?.granted ? (
            <CameraView ref={cameraRef} style={styles.cameraView} facing="front">
              <View style={styles.ovalOverlayContainer}>
                <View style={styles.ovalFrame} />
              </View>
            </CameraView>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.permissionFallback}>
              <Ionicons name="camera-outline" size={48} color="#FFFFFF" />
              <Text style={styles.permissionText}>Camera access is needed for facial verification.</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity style={styles.grantBtn} onPress={requestPermission}>
                <Text style={styles.grantBtnText}>Enable Camera</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        {/* Soft Instruction Banner */}
        <View style={[styles.instructionBox, { backgroundColor: palette.primarySoft }]}>
          <Text style={[styles.instructionText, { color: palette.textPrimary }]}>
            {statusMessage}
          </Text>
        </View>

        {/* Primary Capture Button */}
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.captureBtn, { backgroundColor: palette.primary }, submitting && { opacity: 0.7 }]}
          onPress={handleCaptureAndVerify}
          disabled={submitting}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
        >
          {submitting ? (
            <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#FFFFFF" />
          ) : (
            <>
              <Ionicons name="camera-outline" size={19} color="#FFFFFF" />
              <Text style={styles.captureBtnText}>Capture and save</Text>
            </>
          )}
        </TouchableOpacity>

        {/* Secondary Cancel Button */}
        {onBack && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.cancelBtn, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderColor: palette.border }]}
            onPress={onBack}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.cancelBtnText, { color: palette.primary }]}>Cancel</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1 },
  royalHeader: {
    paddingHorizontal: 18,
    paddingBottom: 22,
    borderBottomLeftRadius: 28,
    borderBottomRightRadius: 28,
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  backCircle: {
    width: 40,
    height: 40,
    borderRadius: 20,
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  headerEyebrow: {
    fontSize: 11,
    fontWeight: '800',
    color: '#DBE4FF',
    letterSpacing: 0.8,
  },
  headerTitle: {
    fontSize: 24,
    fontWeight: '800',
    color: '#FFFFFF',
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  headerSub: {
    fontSize: 13,
    color: '#E0E7FF',
    marginTop: 2,
  },
  body: {
    flex: 1,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingTop: 14,
    paddingBottom: 110,
    gap: 10,
  },
  cameraCard: {
    flex: 1,
    borderRadius: 24,
    overflow: 'hidden',
    backgroundColor: '#1E293B',
  },
  cameraView: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  ovalOverlayContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',

  },

    width: 220,

    backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  },
  permissionFallback: {
    flex: 1,

  },

    color: '#FFFFFF',

    paddingVertical: 12,
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    borderRadius: 14,
  },
  instructionText: {
    fontSize: 13,
    fontWeight: '600',
    lineHeight: 18,
  },
  captureBtn: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    gap: 8,
    height: 50,
    borderRadius: 14,
  },
  captureBtnText: {
    color: '#FFFFFF',
    fontSize: 15,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
  cancelBtn: {
    height: 48,
    borderRadius: 14,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },

    fontSize: 15,
    fontWeight: '800',
  },
});

export default ScannerScreen;

# FILE: mobile\src\screens\SettingsScreen.tsx

/**
 * AttendSure - User Profile & Application Settings Screen
 * File: mobile/src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  ScrollView,
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { Image } from 'expo-image';
import QRCode from 'react-native-qrcode-svg';
import * as ImagePicker from 'expo-image-picker';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { getServerBaseUrl } from '../services/configService';

export interface ExtendedUserProfile {
  id?: number | string;
  username?: string;
  first_name?: string;
  last_name?: string;
  middle_name?: string;
  suffix?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  contact_number?: string;
  phone?: string;
  position?: string;
  department?: string;
  role?: string;
  employee_id?: string;
  faculty_id?: string | number;
  photo?: string | null;
  photo_url?: string | null;
}

export const SettingsScreen: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, logout, updateUser } = useAuth();

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<ExtendedUserProfile | null>(user as ExtendedUserProfile | null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [serverBase, setServerBase] = useState<string>('');

  // Editable user fields
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState<string>(user?.first_name || '');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState<string>(user?.last_name || '');
  const [contactNumber, setContactNumber] = useState<string>(user?.contact_number || '');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState<string>(user?.email || '');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState<string>('');

  // Profile photo state
  const [newPhotoBase64, setNewPhotoBase64] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const resolvePhotoUri = useCallback(
    (url?: string | null): string | undefined => {
      if (!url) return undefined;
      if (url.startsWith('http://') || url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('data:image/')) {
        return url;
      }
      return `${serverBase.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/${url.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`;
    },
    [serverBase]
  );

  // Automatically fetch profile information in the background
  const fetchProfile = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await apiClient.get<ExtendedUserProfile>('me/');
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        setFirstName(res.data.first_name || '');
        setLastName(res.data.last_name || '');
        setContactNumber(res.data.contact_number || res.data.phone || '');
        setEmail(res.data.email || '');
      }
    } catch {
      // Keep session state intact
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    getServerBaseUrl().then((url) => setServerBase(url));
    fetchProfile();
  }, [fetchProfile]);

  // Pick new profile picture with size validation
  const handlePickPhoto = async () => {
    try {
      const { status } = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (status !== 'granted') {
        Alert.alert('Permission Needed', 'Please allow photo library access in phone settings.');
        return;
      }

      const result = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ['images'],
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
        quality: 0.6,
        base64: true,
      });

      if (!result.canceled && result.assets && result.assets[0]) {
        const asset = result.assets[0];

        // Cybersecurity check: file size limit 5 MB
        if (asset.fileSize && asset.fileSize > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
          Alert.alert('File Too Large', 'Please select a photo smaller than 5 MB.');
          return;
        }

        if (asset.base64) {
          setNewPhotoBase64(`data:image/jpeg;base64,${asset.base64}`);
          setPhotoPreview(asset.uri);
        } else {
          setPhotoPreview(asset.uri);
        }
      }
    } catch {
      Alert.alert('Photo Error', 'Unable to open photo picker.');
    }
  };

  // Save personal updates to the database
  const handleSaveProfile = async () => {
    if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim()) {
      Alert.alert('Missing Name', 'First name and last name are required.');
      return;
    }

    if (newPassword.trim() && newPassword.trim().length < 8) {
      Alert.alert('Weak Password', 'Password must be at least 8 characters long.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const payload: Record<string, string> = {
        first_name: firstName.trim(),
        last_name: lastName.trim(),
        contact_number: contactNumber.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
      };

      if (newPassword.trim()) {
        payload.password = newPassword.trim();
      }

      if (newPhotoBase64) {
        payload.photo = newPhotoBase64;
      }

      const res = await apiClient.put('me/', payload);
      if (res.data) {
        setProfile(res.data);
        updateUser(res.data);
      }

      setNewPassword('');
      setNewPhotoBase64(null);
      Alert.alert('Success', 'Your personal information has been saved.');
    } catch (err: any) {
      const msg = err?.response?.data?.error || err?.response?.data?.detail || 'Failed to update personal details.';
      Alert.alert('Update Failed', msg);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const qrPayload = JSON.stringify({
    app: 'ATTENDSURE_PASS',
    id: profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || 'USER',
    name: profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim(),
    role: profile?.role || 'FACULTY',
  });

  const displayPhoto = photoPreview || resolvePhotoUri(profile?.photo_url || profile?.photo);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['left', 'right']}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollContent} showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}>
        {/* Profile Header & Photo Manager */}
        <View style={styles.profileHeaderCard}>
          <View style={styles.avatarWrapper}>
            <View style={styles.avatarCircle}>
              {displayPhoto ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: displayPhoto }} style={styles.avatarImg} contentFit="cover" />
              ) : (
                <Ionicons name="person" size={40} color="#004b87" />
              )}
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cameraBadgeBtn}
              onPress={handlePickPhoto}
              accessibilityLabel="Change Photo"
            >
              <Ionicons name="camera" size={15} color="#ffffff" />
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.profileName}>
            {profile?.full_name || `${firstName} ${lastName}`.trim() || 'User Account'}
          </Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileRole}>{profile?.position || profile?.role || 'Staff Member'}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.profileDept}>{profile?.department || 'Academic Department'}</Text>

          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.idChip}>
              <Ionicons name="id-card-outline" size={12} color="#004b87" />
              <Text style={styles.idChipText}>ID: {profile?.employee_id || profile?.username || '—'}</Text>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.idChip}>
              <Ionicons name="shield-checkmark-outline" size={12} color="#059669" />
              <Text style={[styles.idChipText, { color: '#059669' }]}>Verified Account</Text>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* Digital School Clearance Pass */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Digital Gate Pass</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Check in and out using facial recognition and campus GPS boundary verification.</Text>
          <View style={styles.qrContainer}>
            <QRCode value={qrPayload} size={140} color="#004b87" backgroundColor="#ffffff" />
          </View>
        </View>

        {/* User Information Form */}
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.cardTitle}>Personal Information</Text>
          <Text style={styles.cardSubtitle}>Manage your name, contact phone, email, and password.</Text>

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>First Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={firstName}
            onChangeText={setFirstName}
            placeholder="Enter first name"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Last Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={lastName}
            onChangeText={setLastName}
            placeholder="Enter last name"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Email Address</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={email}
            onChangeText={setEmail}
            keyboardType="email-address"
            autoCapitalize="none"
            placeholder="name@school.edu"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>Mobile Phone Number</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={contactNumber}
            onChangeText={setContactNumber}
            keyboardType="phone-pad"
            placeholder="09XXXXXXXXX"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <Text style={styles.inputLabel}>New Password (leave blank to keep current)</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={newPassword}
            onChangeText={setNewPassword}
            secureTextEntry
            placeholder="Minimum 8 characters"
            placeholderTextColor="#94a3b8"
          />

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.saveBtn, saving && { opacity: 0.6 }]}
            onPress={handleSaveProfile}
            disabled={saving}
            activeOpacity={0.8}
          >
            {saving ? (
              <ActivityIndicator size="small" color="#ffffff" />
            ) : (
              <Text style={styles.saveBtnText}>Save Changes</Text>
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        {/* Sign Out Button */}
        <TouchableOpacity style={styles.logoutBtn} onPress={logout} activeOpacity={0.8}>
          <Ionicons name="log-out-outline" size={18} color="#dc2626" />
          <Text style={styles.logoutBtnText}>Sign Out</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
};

/**
 * AttendSure - Mobile Server Discovery & URL Security Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/configService.ts
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

export const SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY = 'attendsure_server_base_url';
const CACHE_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;

export const ENDPOINTS = {
  health: '/health/db/',
  auth: {
    login: '/auth/login/',
    logout: '/auth/logout/',
    userProfile: '/me/',
  },
  classroom: {
    studentList: '/classroom/student_list/',
    classList: '/classroom/class_list/',
    takeAttendance: '/classroom/batch-scan/',
  },
  schoolGate: {
    tap: '/gate/scan/',
    logs: '/gate/logs/',
    verify: '/gate/verify/',
  },
  telemetry: {
    heartbeat: '/telemetry/heartbeat/',
    geofenceStatus: '/geofence/',
  },
  reports: {
    dtr: '/dtr/',
    sf1: (sectionId: number | string) => `/reports/sf1/${sectionId}/`,
    sf2: (sectionId: number | string) => `/reports/sf2/${sectionId}/`,
    sf4: '/reports/sf4/',
  },
};

export function cleanUrl(rawUrl?: string | null): string {
  if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== 'string') {
    return Platform.OS === 'android' ? 'http://10.0.2.2:8000' : 'http://127.0.0.1:8000';
  }
  let trimmed = rawUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '').replace(/\/api$/i, '').replace(/\/+$/, '');
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) {
    trimmed = `http://${trimmed}`;
  }
  return trimmed;
}

export const sanitizeUrl = cleanUrl;

export function isValidServerUrl(url?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') return false;
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed.startsWith('http') ? trimmed : `http://${trimmed}`);
    return parsed.protocol === 'http:' || parsed.protocol === 'https:';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function getMetroHostIp(): string | null {
  try {
    const Constants = require('expo-constants').default;
    const hostUri =
      Constants?.expoConfig?.hostUri ||
      Constants?.manifest?.debuggerHost ||
      Constants?.manifest2?.extra?.expoGo?.debuggerHost;
    if (hostUri && typeof hostUri === 'string') {
      const ip = hostUri.split(':')[0];
      if (ip && ip !== 'localhost' && ip !== '127.0.0.1' && !ip.includes('expo.app')) {
        return ip;
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return null;
}

function loadRootEndpoints(): { active_tunnel_url?: string; lan_servers?: string[]; production_domain?: string } {
  try {
    return require('../../../endpoints.json');
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export function getCandidateServers(): string[] {
  const candidates: string[] = [];
  const rootEndpoints = loadRootEndpoints();
  const isNativeMobile = Platform.OS === 'android' || Platform.OS === 'ios';

  // 1. Local Wi-Fi LAN servers first (fastest direct connection on school Wi-Fi)
  if (Array.isArray(rootEndpoints.lan_servers)) {
    for (const lan of rootEndpoints.lan_servers) {
      if (lan && typeof lan === 'string') {
        candidates.push(cleanUrl(lan));
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Environment variable from mobile/.env
  const envUrl = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL;
  if (envUrl && envUrl.trim().length > 0) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(envUrl));
  }

  // 3. Dynamic laptop IP from Expo Metro bundler
  const metroIp = getMetroHostIp();
  if (metroIp) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(`http://${metroIp}:8000`));
  }

  // 4. Public Cloudflare Tunnel (ignore 127.0.0.1/localhost on physical phones)
  if (rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url) {
    const tunnel = cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.active_tunnel_url);
    const isLoopback = tunnel.includes('127.0.0.1') || tunnel.includes('localhost');
    if (!isNativeMobile || !isLoopback) {
      candidates.push(tunnel);
    }
  }

  // 5. Production school domain
  if (rootEndpoints.production_domain) {
    candidates.push(cleanUrl(rootEndpoints.production_domain));
  }

  // 6. Web/Emulator fallback only
  if (!isNativeMobile) {
    candidates.push('http://127.0.0.1:8000');
  }

  return Array.from(new Set(candidates.filter((u) => Boolean(u && isValidServerUrl(u)))));
}

export function getDefaultBaseUrl(): string {
  return getCandidateServers()[0] || (Platform.OS === 'android' ? 'http://10.0.2.2:8000' : 'http://127.0.0.1:8000');
}

export const DEFAULT_SERVER_URL = getDefaultBaseUrl();

let cachedUrl: string | null = null;
let lastCacheTime = 0;

export function invalidateServerCache(): void {
  cachedUrl = null;
  lastCacheTime = 0;
}

export async function resolveFastestServer(): Promise<string> {
  const candidates = getCandidateServers();
  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    cachedUrl = getDefaultBaseUrl();
    return cachedUrl;
  }

  const probeServer = async (targetUrl: string): Promise<string> => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2200);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${targetUrl}/api/health/db/`, {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      if (res.status >= 200 && res.status < 500) return targetUrl;
      throw new Error(`Status ${res.status}`);
    } catch (err) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      throw err;
    }
  };

  try {
    const fastestUrl = await Promise.any(candidates.map((url) => probeServer(url)));
    cachedUrl = fastestUrl;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, fastestUrl).catch(() => {});
    return fastestUrl;
  } catch {
    const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => null);
    const fallback = stored && isValidServerUrl(stored) ? cleanUrl(stored) : candidates[0];
    cachedUrl = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
}

export async function getServerBaseUrl(): Promise<string> {
  if (cachedUrl && Date.now() - lastCacheTime < CACHE_TTL_MS) {
    return cachedUrl;
  }
  return resolveFastestServer();
}

export async function setServerBaseUrl(newUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!isValidServerUrl(newUrl)) {
    throw new Error('Please enter a valid web address starting with http:// or https://');
  }
  const cleaned = cleanUrl(newUrl);
  try {
    await AsyncStorage.setItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY, cleaned);
    cachedUrl = cleaned;
    lastCacheTime = Date.now();
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function resetServerBaseUrl(): Promise<void> {
  await AsyncStorage.removeItem(SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY).catch(() => {});
  invalidateServerCache();
}

export async function buildApiUrl(endpoint: string): Promise<string> {
  const base = await getServerBaseUrl();
  const cleanEndpoint = endpoint.startsWith('/') ? endpoint : `/${endpoint}`;
  return `${base}/api${cleanEndpoint}`;
}

export async function testServerConnection(
  testUrl?: string
): Promise<{ ok: boolean; message: string; pingMs: number }> {
  const target = cleanUrl(testUrl || cachedUrl || getDefaultBaseUrl());
  const startTime = Date.now();

  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);
    const response = await fetch(`${target}/api/health/db/`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    if (response.status >= 200 && response.status < 500) {
      return { ok: true, message: `Connected to school server (${pingMs}ms)`, pingMs };
    }
    return { ok: false, message: `Server replied with status ${response.status}`, pingMs };
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const pingMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    const isAbort = err && typeof err === 'object' && 'name' in err && err.name === 'AbortError';
    return {
      ok: false,
      message: isAbort ? 'Connection timed out. Checking alternative route...' : 'Cannot reach server. Verify network connection.',
      pingMs,
    };
  }
}

export default {
  getServerBaseUrl,
  setServerBaseUrl,
  resetServerBaseUrl,
  resolveFastestServer,
  invalidateServerCache,
  testServerConnection,
  buildApiUrl,
  isValidServerUrl,
  cleanUrl,
  sanitizeUrl,
  getDefaultBaseUrl,
  SERVER_URL_STORAGE_KEY,
  DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
  ENDPOINTS,
};

# FILE: mobile\src\services\deviceService.ts

/**
 * AttendSure - Hardware Device Identity Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/deviceService.ts
 *
 * Cybersecurity & Anti-Cheating:
 * 1. Cryptographic Entropy: Generates unguessable device IDs using expo-crypto.
 * 2. Hardware Enclave & Keystore: Stores IDs using AES-256 encrypted SecureStore.
 * 3. In-Memory Caching: Speeds up repeated lookups without disk latency.
 * 4. Decoupled Architecture: Completely breaks require cycles between screens and background tasks.
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';

export const SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY = 'attendsure_hardware_device_uuid';

// In-memory cache to prevent repeated storage disk reads
let cachedDeviceUuid: string | null = null;

/**
 * Generates or retrieves the hardware-encrypted device UUID.
 */
export const getOrCreateDeviceUuid = async (): Promise<string> => {
  if (cachedDeviceUuid) {
    return cachedDeviceUuid;
  }

  try {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      const stored = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY) : null;
      if (stored) {
        cachedDeviceUuid = stored;
        return stored;
      }
      const webUuid = `DEV-WEB-${Crypto.randomUUID ? Crypto.randomUUID().substring(0, 13).toUpperCase() : Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10).toUpperCase()}`;
      if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, webUuid);
      }
      cachedDeviceUuid = webUuid;
      return webUuid;
    }

    const existing = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY);
    if (existing) {
      cachedDeviceUuid = existing;
      return existing;
    }

    // Generate secure random entropy for hardware binding
    const randomBytes = await Crypto.getRandomBytesAsync(8);
    const hex = Array.from(randomBytes)
      .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
      .join('')
      .toUpperCase();

    const generated = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-${hex.substring(0, 8)}-${hex.substring(8, 16)}`;
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(SECURE_DEVICE_UUID_KEY, generated, {
      keychainAccessible: SecureStore.ALWAYS_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY,
    });

    cachedDeviceUuid = generated;
    return generated;
  } catch (error) {
    const fallback = `DEV-${Platform.OS.toUpperCase()}-TEMP-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
    cachedDeviceUuid = fallback;
    return fallback;
  }
};

import { apiClient } from '../api/client';
import { ENDPOINTS } from '../api/endpoints';
import { SchoolProfile, AttendanceLog } from '../types';

export async function fetchSchoolSettings(): Promise<SchoolProfile> {
  const response = await apiClient.get<SchoolProfile>(ENDPOINTS.SCHOOL_PROFILE);
  return response.data;
}

export async function fetchTodayGatePunches(facultyId?: number): Promise<AttendanceLog[]> {
  const params: Record<string, any> = {};
  if (facultyId) {
    params.faculty_id = facultyId;
  }
  const response = await apiClient.get<AttendanceLog[] | { results: AttendanceLog[] }>(
    ENDPOINTS.ATTENDANCE_LOGS,
    { params }
  );
  
  if (Array.isArray(response.data)) {
    return response.data;
  }
  return response.data.results || [];
}

# FILE: mobile\src\services\pushService.ts

/**
 * AttendSure - Safe Push & Wake-Up Service
 * File: mobile/src/services/pushService.ts
 *
 * Cybersecurity & Performance:
 * 1. Expo Go Detection: Safely skips remote push registration in the Expo Go sandbox to eliminate SDK 53 console warnings.
 * 2. Standalone APK Ready: Enables high-priority wake-up push notifications in production builds.
 * 3. LogBox Cleanliness: Filters known sandbox notices from polluting the terminal.
 */

import { Platform, LogBox } from 'react-native';
import Constants, { ExecutionEnvironment } from 'expo-constants';
import defaultApiClient, { apiClient as namedClient } from '../api/client';
import {
  startPassivePresenceTracking,
  stopPassivePresenceTracking,
} from './backgroundLocation';

// Safely resolve HTTP client
const client = defaultApiClient || namedClient;

export const BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK = 'ATTENDSURE_BACKGROUND_PUSH_TASK';

// Ignore the Expo Go sandbox push warning in development
LogBox.ignoreLogs([
  '[expo-notifications] Push notifications (remote notifications) are unavailable in Expo Go',
]);

// Check if running inside Expo Go sandbox
const isRunningInExpoGo =
  Constants.executionEnvironment === ExecutionEnvironment.StoreClient;

let Notifications: any = null;
let TaskManager: any = null;

// Only initialize remote push listeners if NOT in Expo Go sandbox
if (!isRunningInExpoGo) {
  try {
    Notifications = require('expo-notifications');
    TaskManager = require('expo-task-manager');

    if (Notifications?.setNotificationHandler) {
      Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
        handleNotification: async () => ({
          shouldShowAlert: false,
          shouldPlaySound: false,
          shouldSetBadge: false,
          shouldShowBanner: false,
          shouldShowList: false,
        }),
      });
    }

    if (TaskManager?.defineTask) {
      TaskManager.defineTask(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK, async ({ data, error }: any) => {
        if (error) return;
        const action = data?.notification?.data?.action?.toUpperCase();
        if (action === 'START_TRACKING_IN') {
          await startPassivePresenceTracking();
        } else if (action === 'STOP_TRACKING_OUT') {
          await stopPassivePresenceTracking();
        }
      });

      Notifications.registerTaskAsync(BACKGROUND_NOTIFICATION_TASK).catch(() => {});
    }
  } catch (err) {
    // Suppress if native dependencies are absent
  }
}

/**
 * Registers device push token with the backend.
 * Safely returns null in Expo Go and uses background GPS telemetry instead.
 */
export async function registerPushTokenWithBackend(): Promise<string | null> {
  if (isRunningInExpoGo || !Notifications?.getExpoPushTokenAsync) {
    console.log('[PushService] Operating in Expo Go: using automated background GPS telemetry.');
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const { status: existingStatus } = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    let finalStatus = existingStatus;

    if (existingStatus !== 'granted') {
      const { status } = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();
      finalStatus = status;
    }

    if (finalStatus !== 'granted') {
      return null;
    }

    const pushTokenData = await Notifications.getExpoPushTokenAsync();
    const token = pushTokenData?.data;

    if (!token) return null;

    const targetUrl = client?.defaults?.baseURL?.endsWith('/api')
      ? '/users/register-push-token/'
      : '/api/users/register-push-token/';

    await client.post(targetUrl, {
      push_token: token,
      platform: Platform.OS,
    });

    console.log('[PushService] Production push token bound successfully.');
    return token;
  } catch (err: any) {
    return null;
  }
}
